### [1] [Verifier-First Evaluation of Agentic LLMs for Infrastructure-as-Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20478)
*Mohamed Jouini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究自然语言到Terraform基础设施即代码生成，比较七种智能体策略在186个AWS/Terraform任务上的可验证通过率。结果显示，主动检索、验证器反馈迭代和反思式提示优化均显著提升性能；政策文本缺失是后期OPA验证失败的主要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IaC生成不仅需要语法正确，还必须满足云服务提供商模式、资源依赖关系及组织约束。现有评估若只考察文本或语法质量，无法定位配置在验证、规划和策略合规环节的真实失效原因，因此需要以验证器为中心的系统比较。

Method: 作者在IaC-Eval v2基准上评估七种Terraform生成策略，覆盖186个AWS任务并使用Rego v1意图约束。评测将失败分为terraform validate、terraform plan和opa eval三个阶段；所有两两策略比较使用McNemar检验，并报告Wilson置信区间，显著性水平为0.05。比较方法包括主动RAG、验证反馈迭代修正、GEPA反思式指令优化、SIMBA无教师示例注入，以及向模型显式提供Rego策略文本的诊断实验。

Result: 主动RAG将Qwen2.5-Coder 7B的pass@1从14.0%提高到45.7%，主要原因是VALIDATE_FAIL任务数从144降至66，差异显著。验证器反馈迭代使Qwen 7B达到62.9%、GPT-4o达到84.4%，且呈现明显的二元收敛现象：任务通常在一次重试中解决，否则耗尽预算。GEPA仅使用80次验证器引导轨迹，即在主动RAG基线上提升7.5个百分点。SIMBA达到与主动RAG统计等价的效果，但未能解决占其失败案例50%的SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY错误。诊断实验表明，迭代修正后79%的OPA失败属于信息缺口型失败，在可见策略文本时可被解决。

Conclusion: 验证驱动的生成与修正是提升IaC可靠性的关键，其中迭代反馈取得最高通过率，检索和提示优化可显著降低前期模式验证错误。后期策略合规失败更多源于模型未获得约束信息，而非单纯推理不足；因此，未来IaC智能体应将可访问的策略上下文、结构化验证反馈和针对自定义属性的约束建模作为核心能力。

Abstract: Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC) generation from natural language requires satisfying provider schemas, dependency planning, and organizational policy constraints, not merely producing syntactically plausible configurations. We present a verifier-first empirical study of seven agentic strategies for Terraform generation evaluated on IaC-Eval v2, a modernized 186-task AWS/Terraform benchmark with Rego v1 intent policies. Our evaluation separates failures into three verifier stages (terraform validate, terraform plan, opa eval) and applies McNemar's test with Wilson confidence intervals on all pairwise comparisons (n=186, alpha=0.05).
  We report five principal findings. (1) Active retrieval via ReAct agents with MCP or ChromaDB-backed RAG raises Qwen2.5-Coder 7B from 14.0% to 45.7% pass@1 (p<0.0001), primarily by reducing VALIDATE_FAIL from 144 to 66 tasks. (2) Iterative refinement with verifier feedback achieves 62.9% (Qwen 7B) and 84.4% (GPT-4o) pass@1, exhibiting binary convergence -- tasks either resolve in one retry or exhaust the budget. (3) GEPA reflective instruction optimization raises the Active RAG baseline by +7.5 pp (p=0.026) using only 80 verifier-guided rollouts, providing evidence that prompt optimizers can improve verifiable IaC generation without weight updates. (4) SIMBA teacher-free demonstration injection achieves performance equivalent to Active RAG (p=1.0) without retrieval infrastructure, but fails to address the dominant SELF_DEFINED_PROPERTY error class (50% of failures). (5) A diagnostic Rego-injection experiment shows that 79% of post-refinement OPA failures are information-gap failures resolvable when policy text is visible (p=0.016), motivating policy

</details>


### [2] [Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation: to Enable More Secure Software Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20933)
*Jesse Phillips,Tracy Hall,Paul Rayson,Mo El-Haj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出“Transformer辅助的基于LLM的源代码摘要”方法：将任务专用Transformer生成的摘要作为提示信息输入LLM，以结合Transformer的词汇对齐优势与LLM的语义生成能力。实验表明，该方法的BLEU-4提升7.8%，另有一项指标提升5%，但摘要未说明该指标名称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码摘要有助于开发与维护人员理解代码，但实际项目中常存在摘要缺失、不完整或过时的问题。任务专用Transformer在BLEU等依赖词汇重叠的指标上表现较好，却未必能反映真实摘要质量；LLM具备更强的语义理解与抽象生成能力，但其输出措辞常与人工参考摘要差异较大，因而在传统NLG指标上的得分偏低。论文旨在弥合二者间的这一矛盾。

Method: 研究选取四个LLM，并设计四种不同提示模板。在提示中嵌入由任务专用Transformer生成的代码摘要，使其充当LLM摘要生成时的辅助上下文或候选信息。该提示工程策略利用Transformer提供的参考措辞与结构线索，同时由LLM进行语义整合和自然语言生成。

Result: 作者观察到Transformer辅助提示能够提升生成摘要在自动评价中的表现，其中BLEU-4相对提升7.8%；摘要还报告另一项指标提升5%，但未给出该指标的具体名称、实验数据集、各LLM的独立结果、比较基线及统计显著性信息。因此，现有摘要只能支持该方法具有正向效果，尚不足以判断其跨模型和跨项目的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，采用任务专用Transformer的输出增强LLM提示，是改善源代码摘要的一种可行混合方案。其核心贡献在于将传统模型的词汇匹配能力与LLM的语义生成能力结合。后续应补充人工质量评估、事实一致性评估、不同模型与数据集上的消融实验，以及对自动指标与实际开发者效用之间关系的验证。

Abstract: Neural Source Code Summarisation (NSCS) aims to generate natural language summaries of source code to improve developers' and maintainers' understanding of code. Source code summaries are vital during the maintenance phase of the Secure Software Development Lifecycle (SSDLC), as they improve maintainers' understanding of code and help reduce the number of bugs and vulnerabilities in a software system. However, summaries are often missing, incomplete, or outdated in many software systems. Solutions to this problem use small, task-specific Transformer models or code-aware Large Language Models (LLMs). Task-specific Transformer-generated summaries often score well across many natural language generation (NLG) metrics, but these metrics reward lexical overlap rather than summary quality. Conversely, the ability of LLMs to capture semantics and produce high-quality summaries presents an exciting solution to this problem. This is especially relevant given the increased availability of LLMs and improvements in workstation hardware in recent years, which mean that some LLMs can now be run on developers' workstations. However, because of their abstractive nature, LLM-generated code summaries often differ greatly from developer-written summaries in the words and phrases they use, resulting in low scores across NLG metrics. We show how combining these two methods, by using Transformer-generated summaries in prompt engineering, may enable LLMs to create better source code summaries and help software practitioners maintain secure systems. We prompt four LLMs using four different prompts, with a task-specific Transformer used to assist the LLMs within the prompts. We present "Transformer-Assisted LLM-Based Source Code Summarisation", a method through which we observe an improvement of 7.8% in BLEU-4 and 5%.

</details>


### [3] [Maintenance Signals in AI-Assisted GitHub Repositories: Evidence from GenAI Adopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21079)
*Rikuto Tsuchida,Youmei Fan,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Gema Rodríguez-Pérez,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究考察生成式人工智能辅助开发在GitHub项目中的可观测维护成本信号，发现其可能减少代码生成工作，但会将部分成本转移至文档编写、生成内容验证、调试维护及外部AI服务依赖管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论常强调生成式人工智能提升编码效率，但对其是否以及如何改变软件长期维护负担缺乏实证证据。研究旨在通过公开GitHub数据识别采用AI辅助开发后出现的维护成本变化。

Method: 研究分析了622名公开表明采用生成式人工智能的用户、179个含可见AI辅助配置文件的仓库、179个匹配的传统仓库，以及AI辅助仓库中创建的248个议题。比较内容包括项目类型、README文档特征和议题中与AI技术相关的维护问题。

Result: AI辅助仓库覆盖多种项目类型，其README通常更长，包含更多标题与代码块；传统仓库则包含更多外部链接。与生成式人工智能相关的议题经常涉及外部依赖，例如API速率限制及对AI服务提供商接口的依赖。

Conclusion: 生成式人工智能并非单纯降低开发成本，而可能重构维护工作分配：开发者需要投入更多精力验证生成内容、处理AI特定行为，并管理外部AI平台和接口带来的依赖与约束。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) can reduce code-generation effort, but it may shift work to documentation, validation, debugging, and maintenance. We study observable maintenance-cost signals among GenAI adopters on GitHub by analyzing 622 users who publicly signal adoption, 179 repositories with visible AI-assistance configuration files, 179 matched traditional repositories, and 248 issues created in AI-assisted repositories. AI-assisted repositories span diverse project types and contain longer README files with more headers and code blocks, while traditional repositories contain more external URLs. Issues concerning GenAI technology often involve external dependencies, such as API rate limits and reliance on GenAI provider APIs. These findings suggest that AI assistance shifts maintenance costs toward verifying generated content, managing external AI dependencies, and validating AI-specific behavior.

</details>


### [4] [Improving Communication of Changes in Model-Based Engineering with Model-Independent Change Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21084)
*Philip Ochs,Lars Gesmann,Tobias Pett,Ina Schaefer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出将增量建模中形式化定义的模型变更自动映射为模型无关的自然语言变更描述，以提升模型驱动工程中跨学科团队对变更的理解与沟通效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科工程团队依赖模型协作，但不同学科的工程师难以理解彼此模型中的变更。非正式语言描述虽易读，却缺乏客观性和标准化；形式化变更表示虽精确，却难以被人直接理解。因此，需要一种同时保留变更语义、避免模型特定知识依赖并具备可解释性的描述机制。

Method: 作者以增量建模中的形式化变更表示为输入，设计函数将其映射为模型无关语言中的变更描述。评估采用混合方法：通过实现框架验证技术可行性，通过案例研究评估技术适用性，并借助用户研究定性考察描述结果的合理性、实际适用性和可扩展性。

Result: 实现与案例研究表明，该方法在技术上可行，并能够应用于实际模型变更场景。用户研究显示，自动生成的模型无关描述在合理性和实践应用方面具有积极潜力，同时具备进一步扩展的可能。

Conclusion: 该研究为模型驱动工程中的自动化、模型无关变更说明提供了有前景的起点。其核心贡献在于连接形式化变更语义与人类可理解的语言表达，从而支持跨学科协作；但摘要未给出与基线方法的对比结果、生成描述的准确率及适用边界，仍需进一步实证验证。

Abstract: In model-based engineering, inter-disciplinary teams collaborate through models, which change over time for purposes of system development, what makes the proper description of such changes crucial for engineers. However, any change made by the engineer of one discipline will be difficult to understand by the engineers of other disciplines. To overcome this limitation, model-independent change descriptions can be derived instead, which preserve semantics of the changes and do not require model-specific knowledge. The two opposing approaches here are to describe changes using either informal language or formal notions of change. While informal language lacks objectivity and standardisation, formal notions of change lack human interpretability, and thus offering no support for inter-disciplinary communication. In this paper, we propose functions to map formally specified changes, represented in the approach of delta modelling, to change descriptions in model-independent language. In an exhaustive mixed-methods evaluation, we bridge the gap between the theoretical and the practical representation of changes. We quantitatively assess technical feasibility, with an implementation framework, and technical applicability, along a case study; and we qualitatively assess plausibility, practical applicability, and extensibility, in a user study. Our work shows a promising starting point for automated, model-independent description of changes in model-based engineering projects.

</details>


### [5] [Information is all you need: Requirements Engineering Quality Reframed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21319)
*Henning Femmer,Julian Frattini*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种整体性需求工程质量理论，将需求工程视为领域知识的信息粒子在角色与工件之间传递的过程，并通过仿真分析信息流以优化需求工程实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有需求工程质量讨论常笼统强调“上下文”，且工件质量与过程质量视角相对割裂。作者旨在建立统一理论，解释需求工程过程及其产物为何成功或失败。

Method: 理论以离散的“信息粒子”为基本单位，建模其从信息源（如利益相关者）经由需求工件和不同角色，传递至信息目标（如开发者、测试人员）的路径。质量由信息流的有效性与效率共同决定。作者还通过一个敏捷场景的示例性仿真，观察不同流程配置对信息传递的影响。

Result: 仿真表明，即使存在高质量的需求规格说明，在敏捷环境中它仍可能被实际工作流绕过。该结果说明，单独提高工件质量不足以保证需求工程质量；流程中的信息可达性、传递成本和角色间交互同样关键。

Conclusion: 需求工程质量应被理解为信息流质量，而非仅是文档或单个活动的质量。该整体理论能够统一工件与过程视角，并可借助仿真为需求工程流程校准提供可操作的依据。

Abstract: To move beyond this vague appeal to context, this vision proposes a novel holistic theory of requirements engineering (RE) quality. This theory models how information particles, i.e., discrete pieces of domain knowledge, are transferred between roles and artifacts. Since the RE process is ultimately an information transfer, holistic RE quality depends on the properties of information flow, i.e., how effectively and efficiently information is transferred from sources (like stakeholders) to targets (like developers and testers), uniting both artifact- and process-based perspectives on RE quality. In an exemplary simulation of the theory we illustrate why a high-quality specification gets bypassed in an agile context, thereby demonstrating that a simulation can provide actionable insights into calibrating the RE process to optimize the information flow. Beyond organizational applications, we envision that the theory can serve as a coherent theoretical framework for understanding the success or failure of RE processes and artifacts.

</details>


### [6] [Teaching Business Process Modeling to Leverage Soft Skills of Computing Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21344)
*Maria Istela Cagnin,Rick Kazman,Elisa Yumi Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究表明，将行业驱动的项目式业务流程建模教学引入计算机教育，能够在AI辅助软件开发背景下提升学生的职业素养、主动性、动机、沟通与团队协作等软技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统开发需要从业者理解组织业务流程，而AI提升开发效率也加剧了就业竞争。因此，计算机教育除培养技术能力外，还需强化有助于职业竞争力的软技能。

Method: 研究采用行业驱动的项目式教学，组织53名计算机专业学生与利益相关者直接互动，并为真实组织开展业务流程建模。研究系统收集学生反馈，识别其在项目过程中实践和发展的软技能。

Result: 学生反馈显示，业务流程建模教学有效促进了职业性、主动性、学习动机、沟通能力和团队合作能力的发展。与利益相关者协作及面向实际业务问题建模，是软技能培养的重要机制。

Conclusion: 业务流程建模课程不仅能增强学生对业务需求与组织流程的理解，也能系统支持关键软技能发展。该研究为计算机科学教育提供了证据：在AI辅助开发时代，此类课程有助于提升未来从业者的就业准备度与综合职业能力。

Abstract: Background and Context: Organizations have highly depended on software systems to realize their business processes and achieve strategic business goals and competitive advantage; hence, computing professionals who can understand these processes could more effectively build such systems. At the same time, while Artificial Intelligence (AI) has increased the productivity in developing these and other systems, professionals should be better prepared due to the competitiveness of the job market; thus, soft skills could be an important factor to ensure their position in this market. Objective: This paper aims to analyze and present the impact of teaching business process modeling to leverage key soft skills of future computing professionals. Method and Findings: Our method involved an industry-driven, project-based approach with 53 computing students who actively interacted with stakeholders and modeled the organization's business process. We systematically collected the students' feedback, including the soft skills practiced and demonstrating that professionalism, initiative, motivation, communication, and teamwork can be leveraged. Implications: The main implication of this work for the Computer Science Education research community is to provide evidence that teaching business process modeling subjects is particularly beneficial for future professionals, as it could contribute to preparing them regarding their soft skills for the era of AI-assisted software development.

</details>


### [7] [Toward Federated Cognitive Digital Twins over the Edge-to-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21357)
*Alessandra Somma,Alessio Bucaioni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出联邦认知数字孪生（FCDT）架构，将联邦式分布部署与认知推理能力结合，以提升复杂分布式信息物理系统中的可扩展性、实时响应与决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字孪生架构多采用集中式、单体化设计，在分布式场景中容易出现扩展困难、通信时延高和韧性不足等问题；同时，其语义集成与高层推理能力有限。联邦数字孪生虽改善了系统分解和扩展性，但智能能力仍偏向云端集中；认知数字孪生具备推理、可解释性和智能决策优势，却难以直接适配分布式架构。

Method: 作者构建统一的FCDT架构，在边缘到云的连续计算环境中分布智能能力。局部孪生体负责贴近物理实体的实时监测与轻量级认知处理；全局孪生体负责跨系统的语义推理、仿真分析和协同协调。该设计通过多个可交互孪生体实现分布式自治，并以认知机制支持高层决策。

Result: 摘要表明，该架构预期能够降低对单一集中组件的依赖，增强复杂分布式系统的横向扩展能力和响应速度；局部与全局孪生体的分工也有助于兼顾实时处理与系统级优化。摘要未提供实验设置、量化指标或与基线方法的具体性能比较。

Conclusion: FCDT将联邦化部署和认知能力整合为一体，为复杂分布式信息物理系统提供了兼顾自治、实时性和全局智能协调的数字孪生架构方向。其实际有效性仍需通过具体应用场景、通信与计算开销评估，以及定量对比实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are increasingly adopted to monitor, analyze, and optimize Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs) through continuous interaction between physical assets and their digital counterparts. However, current DT architectures often rely on centralized and monolithic designs, leading to scalability, latency, and resilience issues in distributed environment such as smart cities. Moreover, they provide limited support for semantic integration and high-level reasoning, reducing the effectiveness of DT-based decision-making. Recent studies on Federated Digital Twins (FDTs) have addressed scalability by decomposing complex systems into interacting twins, but they still largely centralize intelligence in cloud components. In parallel, Cognitive Digital Twins (CDTs) enhance DTs with semantic reasoning, explainability, and AI-driven decision support, yet they are typically difficult to integrate into distributed architectures. This paper proposes a Federated Cognitive Digital Twin (FCDT) architecture that combines federation and cognition within a unified approach. The architecture distributes intelligence across the edge-to-cloud continuum through local twins, which provide real-time monitoring and lightweight cognitive capabilities, and global twins, which perform system-level reasoning, simulation, and coordination. By integrating distributed autonomy with cognitive reasoning, the proposed approach improves scalability, responsiveness, and decision-making in complex distributed CPSs

</details>


### [8] [From Resource Flow to Executable Tests: Petri-Net-Guided LLM Test Generation for Concurrent Stateful Rust APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21530)
*Kaiwen Zhang,Guanjun Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向并发有状态 Rust 库 API 的测试生成方法：以彩色 Petri 网表达资源、生命周期与因果约束，生成语义合法且覆盖深层状态与并发交错的场景，并将其作为受约束中间表示，驱动大语言模型合成可执行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽能生成 Rust 测试代码，但常出现 API 前置条件不满足、测试路径浅表、并发执行退化为偶然的顺序轨迹等问题。传统模型化与系统测试方法具有较强语义控制能力，却通常需要大量人工将抽象测试场景实现为具体代码。论文旨在弥合形式化场景设计与低成本测试代码具体化之间的差距。

Method: 方法首先使用彩色 Petri 网对 API 资源、所有权、生命周期状态和因果依赖进行建模，其中令牌编码资源及其状态，变迁对应 API 操作。随后从模型中导出三类场景：合法的深状态场景、接近合法的边界场景，以及描述偏序关系的并发场景。这些场景构成约束性中间表示，用于引导大语言模型生成 Rust 测试。为保证生成代码忠实于模型意图，论文引入局部忠实性约定与结构化修复循环；同时通过 Petri 网引导的调度塑形，优先选择高竞争关系的并发骨架进行系统探索。最后，采用分层语义预言机区分代码合成失败与目标 API 实际行为偏离预期的情况。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验数据、基准对象或量化指标。其宣称的能力包括：生成满足模型约束的可执行测试，覆盖深层生命周期状态与近合法边界条件，并以高冲突并发骨架提升对关键交错执行的系统探索能力；分层预言机用于更准确地归因测试失败。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Petri 网的形式化语义约束与大语言模型的代码生成能力结合起来，为并发有状态 Rust API 提供了一条从抽象场景到可执行测试的自动化路径。核心贡献在于以受约束的场景中间表示限制生成过程，并通过修复、调度引导和语义预言机提高测试的合法性、并发有效性与故障归因能力。

Abstract: Concurrent stateful library APIs expose behavior through evolving resource ownership, lifecycle states, and competing interleavings. Large language models can synthesize executable Rust tests, but their outputs often violate API preconditions, remain shallow, or reduce concurrency to accidental sequential traces. Conversely, model-based and systematic testing techniques provide semantic control but commonly require substantial handwritten code to turn abstract scenarios into executable tests. This paper addresses the gap between formal scenario design and low-cost test concretization. We present a Petri-net-guided methodology for test generation over concurrent stateful Rust APIs. The method represents API resources, lifecycle conditions, and causal dependencies as colored tokens and transitions; derives legal deep-state, near-legal, and partial-order concurrent scenarios; and uses these scenarios as a constrained intermediate representation for LLM-based code synthesis. A local-faithfulness contract and structural repair loop preserve the modeled intent during concretization, while Petri-guided schedule shaping prioritizes high-conflict concurrency skeletons for systematic exploration. A layered semantic oracle then distinguishes synthesis failures from violations of the target API's expected behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [9] [Operational Identity: A Finite Audit of Declared and Implemented Rules of Sameness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20729)
*Denise M. Case*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出“声明身份关系”与“实现身份关系”的形式化审计框架，用分区细化关系检测记录系统中声明的同一性规则是否被实际机制忠实执行，并刻画与外部同级身份基准之间的偏离类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记录系统可能公开声明某种实体、事件、范围或规则的同一性判定标准，但其实现机制可在无溯源缺口、无显式矛盾且单条记录均正确的情况下，系统性地采用另一种同一性规则。因此，需要一种可判定的审计方法来识别声明与实现之间的隐性偏离。

Method: 将有限记录域上的声明身份制度建模为共指类分区，将由已披露机制及其类型化身份相关输出诱导的关系建模为操作性身份分区。在分区细化格中比较两者：若声明分区细化于操作分区，即不存在任何声明类被实现机制拆分，则机制具有忠实性。通过枚举记录对，寻找“声明合并而机制分离”的发散见证。若引入的同级基准也拆分声明类，则进一步依据局部分区关系将偏离分类为同级对齐、次同级、超同级或同级不可比；同时将操作分区与同级分区的全局相等单独定义为制度替换。审计结论采用相对于披露工件、评估范围和已识别用途的三值判定，并为各边界提供有限反证见证。

Result: 论文表明，声明与实现关系的偏离可由有限记录对枚举决定；同级对齐仅说明局部关系，不能推出机制实际采用了该同级基准，也不能推出全局制度替换。每次文本编辑均递增的版本字段构成次同级情形：它比声明制度及任一导入基准更细粒度地拆分记录类。通过审计的结论不具单调性，因为扩展转换历史可能合并原有声明类，从而在已检查记录间新产生发散见证。

Conclusion: 该研究将身份审计从文档声明或单记录正确性提升为分区层面的机制—制度一致性检验。其核心贡献是以细化关系、可判定的成对见证和相对化三值结论，严格区分忠实实现、局部同级关系与全局制度替换，并揭示审计通过结论会随历史扩展而失效的非单调性。

Abstract: A record system declares when two records refer to the same entity, occurrence, scope, or rule. Its disclosed implementation mechanisms induce a corresponding operational identity relation. The declared and implemented relations may diverge systematically without producing a provenance gap or detectable contradiction. A system can apply, consistently and with every record individually correct, a rule of sameness that no artifact declares. This paper formalizes that implemented relation. A declared identity regime partitions a finite record domain into co-reference classes; a disclosed mechanism, through its typed identity-relevant outcomes, induces an operational identity partition of the same domain. The audit compares these partitions in the refinement lattice. A mechanism is faithful when the declared partition refines the operational partition, so no declared class is split. A divergence witness is a pair the declaration merges and the mechanism separates; such witnesses are decidable by pair enumeration. When an imported sibling basis also splits a declared class, local comparison with its partition yields sibling-aligned, sub-sibling, super-sibling, or sibling-incomparable divergence. This result reports only the relationship; it does not identify the basis carried by the mechanism. Global equality of the operational and sibling partitions is defined separately as regime substitution and does not follow from sibling alignment. A version field incremented on every textual edit inhabits the sub-sibling case by splitting declared classes more finely than either imported basis. The audit is three-valued and relative to the disclosed artifacts, evaluated surfaces, and identified uses; each boundary has a finite refuting witness. A passing verdict is non-monotone because extending the transformation history can merge declared classes and create a witness among records already examined.

</details>


### [10] [Anti-Goal Reasoning: Rethinking the Theory of Goal Reasoning in Non-Axiomatic Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20902)
*Bowen Xu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出“反目标”概念以明确表示规避意图，并通过心理操作prevent将主动预防与普通目标推理连接起来，避免将“避免G”错误等同于“追求非G”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非公理逻辑中，惯用的¬G!表示法混淆了两种语义：把¬G作为要实现的事件，以及避免G发生。这种混淆可能导致反常推理，即系统仅因行动通常伴随“未受伤”而将规避意图转化为应当行动的正向目标。

Method: 论文从NAL中目标的基本定义出发，扩展出与之对应的反目标定义，使规避可被独立表示而非作为否定事件的追求；随后引入心理操作prevent，用于在需要主动阻止事件时将反目标推理转换并衔接至常规目标推理；最后构造四个最小案例进行规则检验。

Result: 所提出的表示与推理规则能够区分四类情形：追求某事件、被动规避某事件、主动预防某事件，以及通过不采取行动来维持一个期望事件；同时避免了传统¬G!表述引发的悖论性行动结论。

Conclusion: 反目标为NAL中的规避意图提供了不同于否定目标的语义基础。结合prevent操作后，系统能够更一致地处理被动回避与主动预防，提升目标推理在知识和资源不足条件下的表达准确性。

Abstract: Goal reasoning in Non-Axiomatic Logic (NAL) explains how an adaptive system derives means for realizing desired events under insufficient knowledge and resources. However, the representation of avoidance is less clear. A common convention is to express ``avoid $G$'' as the goal sentence ``$\neg G!$'', but this notation conflates two different readings: pursuing the negated event $\neg G$, and avoiding the positive event $G$. This paper shows that the conflation can produce a paradoxical case in which an avoidance intention is converted into a positive goal to act merely because acting is usually followed by the absence of hurt. Starting from NAL's basic definition of goals, the framework is extended with a corresponding definition of anti-goals, so that avoidance can be represented without treating it as the pursuit of a negated event. Finally, a mental operation, $\op{prevent}$, is introduced to connect anti-goal reasoning with ordinary goal reasoning in cases of active prevention. Four minimal case studies check that the resulting rules distinguish pursuit, passive avoidance, active prevention, and withholding action to preserve a desired event.

</details>


### [11] [STLSat---An Improved Tableau for Satisfiability Checking of Signal Temporal Logic Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21081)
*Marco Zamponi,Florian Lammel,Ezio Bartocci,Michele Chiari*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出并证明了一种针对有界离散时间信号时序逻辑（STL）的健全且完备的树形表方法，并实现开源工具STLSat，用于可满足性判定、见证信号合成、蕴含/等价检查及不可满足核提取。实验表明，其组合求解器在扩展基准上可匹配或超过现有工具，同时保持理论正确性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务与安全关键系统常包含大量STL需求，需求间一致性检查、逻辑关系分析和调试成本很高。已有唯一的有界离散时间STL树形表方法对部分公式无法给出可靠的可满足或不可满足结论，限制了基于表方法的自动化需求分析，因此需要修正理论基础并提供实用工具支持。

Method: 作者首先定位已有树形表过程的缺陷，随后设计新的树形表规则，并证明该过程对有界离散时间STL具有健全性与完备性。在工程实现上，开发Rust开源工具STLSat：以新表方法为核心，同时集成增强的一阶逻辑编码与可满足性模理论编码，构成组合式求解框架。工具还支持生成满足公式的具体信号、检查公式蕴含和等价关系，以及提取不可满足核。

Result: 作者发布了扩展公开基准集，覆盖STL和任务时间线性时序逻辑公式。实验显示，STLSat的组合求解模式在整体基准上能够达到或优于现有先进工具的性能；其表方法则为结果正确性提供了健全性保证。

Conclusion: 该工作同时解决了有界离散时间STL树形表既有方法的正确性问题，并给出具备完整需求分析功能的开源求解器。其主要价值在于将经过严格证明的表方法与多种逻辑编码结合，使STL规格的一致性验证、调试和关系分析更加可靠且实用。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a formalism used to describe temporal properties of real-valued signals in cyber-physical systems. In mission- and safety-critical domains, specifications often consist of large collections of STL formulas, making consistency checking and requirement analysis a major engineering bottleneck. Despite tableau-based satisfiability procedures being a natural solution to solve this problem, we have recently found out that the only existing tree-shaped tableau for bounded discrete-time STL does not provide a sound satisfiability/unsatisfiability verdict for all possible STL formulas. In this paper, we pinpoint the flaw in that procedure and present a new tree-shaped tableau which we prove to be sound and complete for bounded discrete-time STL.
  On top of this theoretical foundation, we introduce STLSat, an open-source Rust tool that decides the satisfiability of STL formulas, synthesizes concrete witness signals, checks the logical implication and equivalence between specifications, and extracts unsatisfiable cores, allowing users to identify inconsistent subsets of requirements for more effective specification debugging. STLSat also implements enhanced First-Order Logic and Satisfiability Modulo Theories encodings for STL, which allow it to act as a portfolio solver.
  We evaluate STLSat on an extended benchmark suite (including STL and Mission-time Linear Temporal Logic formulas) that we release publicly. Across the whole benchmark, the portfolio solver matches or outperforms state-of-the-art tools while preserving correctness guaranteed by our sound tableau procedure.

</details>


### [12] [Case study: proving sqrt(2) irrational with LPTP and an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21187)
*Fred Mesnard,Étienne Payet,Wim Vanhoof*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文报告了一个案例研究：借助大语言模型生成部分证明，并使用逻辑程序定理证明器LPTP进行完整校验，从而形式化证明√2不是有理数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索大语言模型在形式化证明中的辅助作用，同时通过可靠的证明器弥补模型可能产生的不严谨或错误，获得人类可读且机器验证的证明。

Method: 作者先定义少量纯逻辑程序谓词，并在以自然演绎为证明语言的LPTP中表达√2无理性的经典证明；随后记录与大语言模型的交互过程，由模型部分参与证明构造，最终将完整证明交由LPTP进行形式检查。

Result: 案例表明，大语言模型能够协助生成形式化证明的部分内容，而LPTP成功验证了最终完整证明。由于LPTP采用自然演绎，其证明过程具有较好的可读性。

Conclusion: 该工作说明了“语言模型生成+定理证明器验证”的协作范式在逻辑程序证明中的可行性：模型可提高证明构造效率，证明器则提供最终正确性保障。摘要未说明该方法对更复杂定理、不同证明系统或自动化程度的泛化效果。

Abstract: We present the interactions with an LLM (Large Language Model) aiming at proving that the square root of 2 is not a rational number in an LP (Logic Programming) context. We start from a few basic pure logic programming predicate definitions. We rely on the LPTP  (Logic Program Theorem Prover) system for stating and proving properties about logic programs.  As the proof language of LPTP  is based on natural deduction,  the proofs are human readable. In our case study, we sketch in LPTP the usual proof showing the irrationality of the square root of 2.  Then we describe the interactions we had with the LLM. We end up with a complete formal proof, partially generated by an LLM and fully proof-checked by LPTP.

</details>


### [13] [Encoding Event-B Proof Rules in Prolog: An Interactive Sequent Prover for ProB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21191)
*Katharina Engels,Jan Gruteser,Michael Leuschel*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文介绍了将600余条Event-B证明规则编码为Prolog，并集成到ProB验证工具中的交互式证明系统。该系统支持证明树可视化、Rodin证明义务导入、多种证明结果导出，以及基于简单启发式的初步自动证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Event-B的证明过程需要兼顾形式化严谨性、可理解性、教学可用性与工具互操作性。既有Java实现较为冗长，不利于维护和扩展；同时，用户需要能够直观检查、控制和复现证明步骤的交互式环境。

Method: 作者以谓词逻辑和集合论为基础，将600多条Event-B证明规则以Prolog规则形式实现，并嵌入ProB。系统通过证明树展示推导过程，允许用户交互式选择规则；支持从Rodin导入证明义务，并导出ProB回放轨迹、可独立浏览的交互式HTML证明树及可回传Rodin的结果。另实现了采用迭代加深搜索和简单启发式策略的初步自动证明器。

Result: Prolog实现相较此前Java版本更紧凑、更易维护且更具扩展性。集成后的工具支持交互式构造和分析证明，尤其适用于教学场景；通过Rodin导入导出机制实现了与既有Event-B工作流的衔接。初步自动证明器能够发现较短证明，但尚未达到高性能自动证明的目标。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用Prolog表达Event-B证明规则能够有效构建透明、可视化且具互操作性的证明环境。其主要贡献在于提升证明规则实现的可维护性，并为交互式教学、证明复现和后续高效自动证明器研发提供基础。

Abstract: Event-B is a formal method rooted in predicate logic and set theory. We encoded over 600 proof rules in Prolog, enabling a systematic, comprehensible proof analysis and construction. By integrating the proof rules into the Prolog-based validation tool ProB, we obtain an interactive proof system with proof tree visualisation. This has advantages in teaching, giving students direct control over the selection of proof rules. Our tool can import proof obligations from the Rodin platform and provides multiple exports: a trace file for proof replay in ProB, an interactive HTML document for tool-independent exploration of the proof tree, and an export back to Rodin, allowing the ProB prover to be used as second chain. Compared to the previous implementation of the proof rules in Java, the encoding in Prolog is more compact, maintainable and extensible. While a preliminary iterative deepening prover with simple heuristics is already available and useful for finding short proofs, we aim to obtain fast automatic provers in the future.

</details>


### [14] [Animation, Verification and Visualisation of Prolog Transition Systems with ProB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21192)
*Jan Gruteser,Michael Leuschel,Katharina Engels,Fabian Vu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文介绍并扩展了ProB的Prolog动画模式，使其能够对由Prolog谓词定义的迁移系统进行交互式执行、验证与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ProB原本已具备模型检验、动画和约束求解能力，但Prolog动画模式在统计仿真、执行轨迹重放、用户交互式迁移及状态展示方面仍需增强，以支持更可靠的验证、教学和策略评估。

Method: 作者梳理现有Prolog动画功能，并实现若干扩展：面向统计检查的仿真、更加可靠的轨迹重放、带用户输入的迁移执行，以及改进的状态可视化。随后将这些能力用于案例研究，重点评估四子棋等游戏中的不同策略。

Result: 扩展后的ProB能够更有效地模拟和分析Prolog定义的状态迁移系统，支持策略比较、交互式演示及更稳健的执行重现。其功能还可服务于Event-B证明义务的新序列证明器，并支持结合交互可视化的教学模型。

Conclusion: ProB的Prolog动画模式经扩展后，成为连接可执行形式化模型、验证分析、策略仿真和可视化教学的更通用工具；其价值不仅体现在游戏案例，也适用于证明辅助和多类交互式形式化建模应用。

Abstract: ProB is a Prolog-based model checker, animator and constraint solver for high-level formal specifications. One can also use ProB to animate transition systems defined by Prolog predicates, allowing the application of its various validation techniques. In this work, we present the existing features of ProB's Prolog animation mode and its recent extensions. The extended capabilities include simulation for statistical checks, more reliable trace replay, transitions with user input and improved state visualisation. We apply the new features to case studies, particularly for evaluating different strategies in game play, such as Connect Four. The features are useful for many other applications, especially for ProB's new sequent prover for Event-B proof obligations, as well as for demonstration models for teaching in combination with interactive visualisation.

</details>


### [15] [What Bugs Do Prolog Students Write? An Empirical Taxonomy and Data-Driven Mutation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21193)
*Ricardo Brancas,Pedro Orvalho,Carolina Carreira,Vasco Manquinho,Ruben Martins*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出LogMorph：一种面向Prolog逻辑程序教学的、由真实学生错误分布驱动的变异生成工具，用于构造更贴近课堂实际的缺陷数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Prolog变异测试框架通常假定各类变异等概率发生，因此生成的故障与学生在学习过程中实际犯错的模式存在偏差，限制了自动反馈工具的训练、评测与实用性。

Method: 研究者分析了265名本科生提交的7,201份Prolog程序，并人工分类其中200份涉及修复的提交，建立细粒度学生错误分类体系。随后开发LogMorph，设计17种按观测错误频率加权采样的变异算子；工具在抽象语法树上定位合法变异位置、按权重选择算子并注入错误；当变异需要新的代码片段时，调用基于SMT的合成器生成片段，最后利用参考测试集验证变异体。

Result: 在16,000个生成变异体的评测中，LogMorph产生的错误类别分布与学生真实错误分布高度一致，多数类别的比例差异控制在两个百分点以内。剩余偏差主要来自与cut相关的变异，以及由SMT合成器生成的代码。

Conclusion: LogMorph证明了利用真实学生数据校准变异算子权重，能够显著提升Prolog教学缺陷数据的真实性。未来可将SMT后端与基于学生代码微调的语言模型结合，以改善代码片段生成质量并进一步缩小分布偏差。

Abstract: Automated feedback tools for logic programming education depend on realistic bug datasets that reflect the mistakes students actually make. However, existing mutation testing frameworks for Prolog treat all mutations as equally likely, producing synthetic faults that diverge from classroom reality. We present an empirical study of 7,201 Prolog submissions from 265 undergraduate students, from which we derive a fine-grained taxonomy of student bugs through manual classification of 200 bug-fixing submissions. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop LogMorph, a data-driven mutation tool whose 17 operators are weighted according to the observed error distribution. LogMorph enumerates valid mutation sites on the abstract syntax tree, samples operators proportionally, injects faults, delegating to an SMT-based synthesizer when new code fragments are needed, and validates each mutant against a reference test suite. An evaluation of 16,000 generated mutants shows that the synthetic error distribution closely matches the student distribution, with most bug categories agreeing to within two percentage points. We identify cut-related mutations and synthesizer-generated code as the main sources of residual divergence, and outline how combining the SMT back-end with a language model fine-tuned on student code can further improve realism.

</details>


### [16] [Chess\_db: A framework for working with large chess game datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21195)
*Nicos Angelopoulos,Jan Wielemaker*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出Chess_db：一套用于处理国际象棋棋局数据的逻辑编程工具，可从PGN棋谱构建后端数据库，并利用开源键值数据库实现对大量局面及其后续统计信息的快速查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管计算机引擎水平已超过顶尖人类棋手，国际象棋训练与研究需求仍持续增长。训练不仅依赖引擎评估，也需要高效检索历史棋局，例如特定棋手的对局记录，以及某一局面下不同后续走法对双方获胜结果的统计关联。

Method: 作者开发Chess_db工具套件，用于在内存中操作棋局并创建数据库后端。系统重点支持解析PGN格式棋谱文件、生成局面数据库，并评估开源键值数据库作为局面表存储方案的适用性，以保存局面相关的棋局记录和结果信息。

Result: 摘要表明，该方案能够从PGN文件构建可查询的棋局数据库，并可借助键值存储实现对覆盖大量棋局的局面信息进行近乎即时的访问。文中未在摘要中给出具体数据规模、查询延迟、存储开销或与其他数据库方案的定量对比。

Conclusion: Chess_db面向国际象棋训练和历史棋谱分析提供了实用的数据管理基础设施。其核心价值在于将PGN棋谱转换为可按局面高效检索的数据库，并验证键值数据库适合承担大规模局面表的快速访问任务。

Abstract: Chess is a two player strategic game that is embedded in classical AI  culture as it was once the frontier for intelligent behaviour.  There was the silent assumption that the advent of computer engines that  play better than the best humans will extinguish interest in the game. However, the opposite has come to pass, with a growing following for the game. A lot of the computational resources are now centered around training of players, where the engine output is just one aspect. Access to  past games is also an essential part, both in knowing what games a specific player has played previously, and also which continuations at a certain position have led to victory more often for each of the two colour players. We present Chess_db a suite of logic programming tools that can effectively manipulate games both in memory and via creating back end databases.  In particular, we provide versatile code that creates databases from PGN (portable game notation) game files and explore the suitability of open source key-value databases for  storing position tables that provide near-instant access to  information pertaining to substantially large number of games.

</details>


### [17] [Case study: solving P-99 with LPTP and an LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21196)
*Fred Mesnard,Thierry Marianne,Étienne Payet,Wim Vanhoof*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文报告了一项针对 P-99 Prolog 练习集前 33 题的实验：研究者通过提示大语言模型生成 Prolog 程序、测试文件及形式化证明，并用 LPTP 对证明进行机器检验，以评估“氛围编程”与“验证驱动编程”结合的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P-99 是经典的 Prolog 练习集合，适合检验模型从自然语言需求生成逻辑程序的能力。仅依赖测试通过无法充分保证逻辑程序的可靠性，因此作者进一步要求模型生成并证明类型正确性、变量实例化性质、终止性、解的唯一性、解的存在性，以及部分程序的函数正确性。

Method: 作者以英文非形式化题目描述为输入，提示 Claude 生成 Prolog 代码和测试文件。随后执行测试，并由模型生成形式化引理与证明；证明目标涵盖类型、groundness、终止性、唯一性、存在性及部分功能正确性。所有证明均使用逻辑程序定理证明器 LPTP 进行验证。研究者还人工检查模型生成的代码、测试、逻辑命题和证明文件，以确认实验结果的有效性与可复现性。

Result: 前 33 个问题被完成，模型共生成 58 个逻辑过程、508 个测试和 257 条引理，形式化证明总计约 11,800 行。作者声称测试得到执行且证明经 LPTP 检查通过，并对每个生成文件进行了人工审阅。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，大语言模型可以在自然语言规格驱动下生成一定规模的 Prolog 程序，并同时产出可由外部证明器检查的可靠性论证。其主要贡献不只是代码生成成功，还在于将自动化测试与形式化验证结合；不过摘要未提供失败案例、提示策略、人工修订程度及与基线方法的定量比较，这些信息将影响对方法泛化能力和自动化程度的判断。

Abstract: Ninety-Nine Prolog Problems (P-99) is a famous set of Prolog exercises. We solved the first thirty three just by prompting an LLM (Large Language Model). We used Claude from Anthropic. By solved we mean: generate the Prolog code and a test file, run the tests and check whether they pass, then formally prove types, groundness, termination, uniqueness, existence and also sometimes functional correctness with LPTP (Logic Program Theorem Prover). Hence our approach is an experiment in vibe-coding/vericoding of P-99. It is a vibe-coding experiment because we started from informal specifications written in English and let Claude generate the Prolog code. It also fits within vericoding because the LLM proved reliability guarantees on the generated Prolog code. Claude wrote 58 logic procedures, 508 tests, 257 lemmas for a total of 11800 proof lines. We manually checked each file generated by the LLM. We checked the Prolog code, ran the tests, examined the logical statements generated by Claude and proof-checked Claude's proofs with LPTP. This paper describes this experiment and provides the main details so that it can be reproduced by the interested reader.

</details>


### [18] [Declarative Problem Solving in UAM Strategic Deconfliction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21197)
*Gioacchino Sterlicchio,Angelo Oddi,Riccardo Rasconi,Francesca Alessandra Lisi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于答案集编程（ASP）的城市空中交通战略冲突消解方法，通过时间同步与航路优化生成无冲突飞行计划，并与约束编程（CP）进行性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空中交通中无人机、空中出租车和直升机数量增长，使空中碰撞、既有航空交通冲突及障碍物冲突风险上升。因而需要能够兼顾安全性与运行效率、且具备可扩展性的战略级冲突消解机制。

Method: 将飞行计划的时间协调、路径选择和冲突约束编码为ASP问题，求解满足无冲突条件的航路与时序方案；同时以CP作为对照，从执行时间、内存资源使用和问题规模扩展性等维度进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明，在小至中等规模实例上，ASP具有更快的执行速度和更好的可扩展性；CP的内存使用较稳定，但随着问题复杂度提高，其求解性能会明显下降。

Conclusion: ASP适合作为城市空中交通战略冲突消解的有效求解框架，尤其适用于中等规模及以下的时间—路径联合优化场景。CP在内存稳定性方面具有优势，但面对更复杂实例时需进一步改进求解效率。

Abstract: The growing demand for Urban Air Mobility (UAM) introduces significant challenges in airspace management, particularly within densely populated metropolitan regions. As the number of aerial vehicles-such as drones, air taxis, and helicopters-continues to rise, so does the risk of mid-air collisions and conflicts with existing air traffic and obstacles. Ensuring safe and efficient UAM operations requires robust strategic deconfliction mechanisms. We propose an Answer Set Programming (ASP) based approach for strategic deconfliction, focusing on time synchronization and route optimization for conflict-free flight plans. The solution is benchmarked against Constraint Programming (CP), emphasizing scalability and resource use. Results show that ASP offers faster execution and better scalability for small to medium cases, while CP maintains stable memory but degrades with complexity.

</details>


### [19] [Walk-In Multi-Stage Patient Flow Scheduling: An ASP Model with DES-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21198)
*Ngoc-Mai Pham,Trang-Linh Nguyen,Thi-Hai-Yen Vuong,Ha-Thanh Nguyen,Van-Giang Trinh*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多科室医院的反应式患者检查流程调度方法：患者动态到达后，仅为新患者确定检查顺序与房间分配，并保持既有安排不变。该方法以ASP/clingo建模，在大规模仿真实验中较贪心基线降低患者停留时间并提高零等待比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院中患者常需在一次就诊中完成多项检查，检查之间存在医学先后依赖，同时受房间容量、跨地点移动及排队等待影响。传统静态排程难以适应实时到达的患者，因此需要一种能够在不扰动已安排患者的前提下，为新到患者快速生成可行且高质量检查路径的调度机制。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为反应式患者流调度：每位新到患者需要决定检查任务的执行序列及各任务的房间指派，约束包括医学优先关系与房间容量限制。采用Answer Set Programming和clingo进行声明式建模与优化，目标函数由两部分构成：连续检查地点间的移动时间，以及按下一项检查时长加权的排队等待时间。为检验服务时长随机性下的鲁棒性，作者额外构建离散事件仿真评估层，并以贪心调度策略作为对比基线。

Result: 在覆盖不同容量配置和患者负载的大规模合成数据实验中，ASP方法相较基于离散事件仿真的基线，能够降低患者停留时间的中位数，并提升无需等待患者的比例。方法优势在高负载条件下最为显著；在各类容量设置中均优于基线，但当容量较高时，改进幅度相对较小。

Conclusion: 基于ASP的反应式调度能够有效处理动态到达、检查依赖和资源容量共同存在的医院患者流程优化问题。其核心价值在于兼顾实时决策与既有计划稳定性，并通过同时优化移动与等待成本改善患者体验和资源利用；尤其在资源紧张、需求较高的场景中具有更强应用潜力。

Abstract: An effective examination and test schedule for patients plays a crucial role in hospital resource management. In this work, we formulate a new reactive patient-flow scheduling problem in multi-department hospitals where walk-in patients arrive over time and each patient requires multiple examinations per visit. Upon each arrival, the scheduler computes a feasible examination pathway-both the sequence of examinations and the room assignment-for the incoming patient only, while previously scheduled assignments remain fixed. This process is subject to medical precedence constraints and room capacity limitations. We model the problem declaratively in Answer Set Programming (ASP) with clingo, and optimize a two-part cost: travel time between consecutive examination locations and queue-induced waiting time, weighted by the duration of the upcoming examination. To assess robustness under stochastic service times, we propose a Discrete-Event Simulation (DES) evaluation layer and a baseline greedy policy for comparison. On large-scale synthetic datasets across various capacity regimes and patient loads, the ASP approach reduces median stay time and increases the proportion of zero-wait patients compared to DES-based baselines. These improvements are most pronounced under heavy load, while the approach still outperforms baselines across all capacity settings, with smaller gains at higher capacities.

</details>


### [20] [Towards a Certifying Grounder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21199)
*Daimy Van Caudenberg,Alexander Ek,Carlos Cantero,Bart Bogaerts*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出CertiFOX，用于有限域上一阶逻辑模型扩展的可认证定界（grounding），通过生成并独立检查定界证明，弥合高层声明式规格与低层求解器输入之间的可信缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定界将含量词的高层理论转换为无量词公式，但通常缺乏认证机制。若该转换过程出错，即使底层求解器给出结果，也无法确认其满足原始规格，因此端到端声明式求解存在信任缺口。

Method: 作者设计CertiFOX框架，包含三部分：一种记录定界推导的证明格式；GroundFOX可认证定界器；以及独立证明检查器CheckFOX。GroundFOX处理一种新提出的定界范式GNF，该范式旨在支持紧凑且具备域感知能力的定界；CheckFOX验证所产生证明，以确认输出的无量词公式与输入规格等价。

Result: 实验表明该方法具有可行性。GroundFOX的定界性能总体可与其他定界器相比，而CheckFOX进行证明检查所带来的额外开销仅为定界时间的较小常数倍。

Conclusion: CertiFOX为定界步骤提供了可验证的等价性保证，使高层一阶逻辑规格到低层求解输入的转换可信，从而为声明式语言的端到端认证求解流程奠定基础。

Abstract: Grounding, the translation of high-level theories into equivalent quantifier-free formulas, is a crucial step in declarative solving, yet it has so far escaped the proof-logging revolution. When this grounding step is not certifying, there is no way of knowing that the obtained solutions actually correspond to the original problem specification, resulting in a trust gap. 
  In this paper, we close the trust gap between the user's high-level specification and the solver's low-level input by introducing a novel certifying grounding framework for first-order logic model expansion (FOX) over finite domains. We present CertiFOX, a framework consisting of: (1) a proof format for grounding derivations, (2) GroundFOX, a certifying grounder operating on theories in Grounding Normal Form (GNF)--a new normal form designed for compact, domain-aware grounding--and (3) CheckFOX, an independent proof checker.
  Our approach guarantees that the grounder's output is equivalent to the input specification, setting the stage for trustworthy end-to-end certified solving pipelines for declarative languages. Experimental evaluation confirms that CertiFOX is a feasible approach. The GroundFOX grounder is broadly comparable with other grounders, and proof checking with CheckFOX adds overhead within a small constant factor of grounding time.

</details>


### [21] [Hybrid MKNF with Classical Negation in the Rule Component](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21202)
*Arun Raveendran Nair Sheela,Christophe Rey,Florence De Grancey*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出对混合MKNF知识库的扩展，使规则组件能够表达经典否定，并给出相应的良基语义与模型计算过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有良基语义下的混合MKNF将描述逻辑与逻辑程序结合，但规则部分不支持经典否定，因而难以直接表示显式的负知识；仅以信息缺失推断否定无法满足需要明确负面事实的推理场景。

Method: 作者扩展混合MKNF语言，在规则组件中引入经典否定；随后形式化定义扩展语言的语法和语义，并设计一个用于计算其良基模型的通用过程。

Result: 该扩展提升了混合MKNF知识库对显式正、负知识的表示能力，同时仍以良基语义作为非单调推理的语义基础，并提供了可操作的模型求解框架。

Conclusion: 通过支持规则中的经典否定，扩展后的混合MKNF弥补了原有形式体系在显式负知识表达上的不足，为描述逻辑与逻辑程序协同推理提供了更具表达力的良基语义框架。

Abstract: Hybrid MKNF knowledge bases under the well-founded semantics integrate Description Logics with Logic Programming. However, they do not support classical negation in the rule component, limiting their ability to represent explicit negative knowledge. This limitation is particularly significant in safety-critical applications, where reasoning often requires explicit negative information rather than interpreting the absence of information as evidence of absence. To address this issue, we introduce an extension of Hybrid MKNF that supports classical negation in the rule component. We formally define the syntax and semantics of the extended language and present a general procedure for computing its well-founded model.

</details>


### [22] [Explainability Framework for Policy-Aware Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21209)
*Heather Merhout,Daniela Inclezan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向具备规则约束策略的智能体的可解释性框架，通过反事实推理生成对比式自然语言解释，并以用户问卷评估解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体进入更多实际场景，仅给出行动结果不足以支持人类理解和信任。尤其是具有规则执行策略的智能体，其行为同时受目标与违规惩罚影响，需要能够说明“为何采取某行动而非另一行动”的解释机制。

Method: 框架借鉴社会科学中的有效解释原则，针对策略感知智能体建模其行动、规则和违规惩罚。系统以Answer Set Programming实现推理，以Python辅助信息抽取和自然语言生成。方法通过构造与原始行动不同的反事实情境，检测替代行动是否会引发违规惩罚或其他不期望事件，并据此生成“采取该行动是因为不采取它会导致事件X”的对比式解释。

Result: 作者实现了该解释生成系统，并通过包含人类参与者的问卷收集对程序生成解释的反馈。摘要表明该框架具备可实现性并完成了初步用户评估，但未报告具体的样本规模、量化指标、统计显著性或与基线方法的比较结果。

Conclusion: 该研究将规则、惩罚和反事实推理结合，为策略约束型智能体提供了可理解的对比式解释路径。其主要价值在于利用违规惩罚识别替代行为的负面后果；不过，框架的实际有效性、泛化性及相对现有解释方法的优势仍需依赖更完整的实验数据验证。

Abstract: In the field of Artificial Intelligence, an agent is a system which is able to autonomously make decisions in order to reach a desired goal. As these systems grow more prevalent in our day-to-day lives, there has been an increased need to add explainability features which can provide an account for an agent's behavior. We therefore propose a framework that outlines how to produce comprehensible explanations for policy-aware agents, or agents which have rule-enforcing policies incorporated in their decision-making framework. This framework is designed using insights from the social sciences on how to produce good explanations. It is implemented in the Answer Set Programming language while using Python to assist with information extraction and natural-language translation. Because these agents incur penalties when violating policies, we are able to leverage these penalties to detect undesirable events in scenarios that are counterfactual to the agents' original actions. This lends itself to creating contrastive explanations (e.g., "the agent performed this action because, had it not, undesirable event X would have occurred."), which formulate the core component for our explainability framework. The framework is evaluated using a survey wherein human participants provide feedback on our program-generated explanations.

</details>


### [23] [Explainable Belief Harmonization under Dynamic Epistemic Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21210)
*Adam Kostka,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出一个用于处理多智能体连续信念配置中“认知划分运行时变化”的混合式形式化框架，结合答案集编程与Python数值计算，以支持观测能力增减导致的表示结构细化或粗化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体信念组合方法通常假设各智能体可表示的信息结构固定，但实际运行中观测能力可能变化，使原本可接受的信念配置变得结构上不可表示。论文旨在解决此类动态结构变化下信念状态的合法性维持、修复及可解释性问题。

Method: 框架将认知划分变化建模为连续信念配置上的拓扑或结构调整：对于划分细化，验证并保持信念配置的可接受性；对于划分粗化，执行唯一的保质量修复。系统采用答案集编程表达声明式完整性约束、违规检测与解释规则，并使用Python完成数值层面的信念质量计算和变换。

Result: 论文给出三项形式化保证：细化操作下的可接受性保持、粗化操作下唯一且质量守恒的修复，以及解释完备性。基于100个随机生成的拓扑变化实例的评估显示，框架实现了完整的违规检测与解释覆盖。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了传统静态信息结构假设，使多智能体信念管理能够适应异构且动态变化的观测分辨率。其主要贡献在于将逻辑约束、数值信念变换和可解释修复统一起来；但摘要未说明计算复杂度、真实任务数据上的效果，以及随机测试的规模和难度分布。

Abstract: Existing approaches to multi-agent belief combination have established mature foundations for combining uncertain beliefs under common assumptions: consensus methods use iterative averaging, logic-based methods resolve conflicting knowledge bases, and epistemic logic analyzes agents' information states. Typically, these approaches assume that the structure determining what each agent can represent remains fixed. However, in many scenarios, agents gain or lose observational capacity during execution, and what was once admissible may become structurally impossible. This paper presents a formal framework for handling such runtime changes in epistemic partitions over continuous belief profiles. A hybrid approach exploits the advantages of answer set programming in elaboration tolerance, declarative integrity constraints, and explanations, with the numerical flexibility of Python. The framework applies to domains where agents operate at heterogeneous and possibly changing levels of resolution, and provides formal guarantees of admissibility preservation under refinement, unique mass-preserving repair under coarsening, and explanation completeness. Evaluation across 100 randomly generated topology changes confirms complete violation detection and explanation coverage.

</details>


### [24] [Scaling Up Formal Representation of Clinical Trial Protocols in Ensemble Logic Using LLMs: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21307)
*Yan Huang,Xubing Hao,Xiaojin Li,Rashmie Abeysinghe,Xiaoqian Jiang,Licong Cui,Guo-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出CT-TEL工作流，利用大语言模型将临床试验方案中的非结构化文本自动转换为时间集成逻辑（TEL）公式，以支持计算化的试验资格判定、队列发现与试验模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验方案通常以自由文本记录，关键的时间表型、动态纳入排除条件及事件时序约束缺乏形式化表达，导致自动推理和规模化分析困难。尽管TEL可表达此类复杂时序规则，人工编码成本高且难以扩展。

Method: 研究构建CT-TEL流水线，使用大语言模型将叙述性临床方案翻译为TEL逻辑公式，并在23项真实临床试验上生成逻辑模型。为评估翻译保真度，采用回译评估：先由模型将TEL公式重新转换为自然语言，再与原始方案文本比较语义相似度。

Result: 在23项试验上的回译语义相似度结果表明，TEL公式能够在一定程度上保留原始方案中的语义信息，说明大语言模型具备从非结构化试验文本提取并映射时序逻辑的潜力。摘要未给出具体的相似度数值、误差类型或与人工标注基线的对比。

Conclusion: CT-TEL为将临床试验方案转化为可计算逻辑提供了可扩展的初步方案，并为后续开展自动化试验模拟和队列识别奠定基础。其证据目前主要来自语义回译一致性；未来仍需以专家标注、逻辑可执行性、临床决策一致性及跨机构泛化能力验证其实际可靠性。

Abstract: The reliance on unstructured free text for documenting clinical trial protocols creates a significant barrier to automated reasoning, cohort discovery, and trial simulation. The lack of formal structure obscures critical temporal phenotypes, such as dynamic eligibility criteria and event timing constraints. Although Temporal Ensemble Logic (TEL) offers an expressive framework for modeling these elements, manual encoding remains a prohibitive bottleneck. We introduce the CT-TEL workflow: a scalable pipeline leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to translate narrative clinical protocols into TEL formulas. We applied CT-TEL to generate logical models for 23 real-world trials from ClinicalTrials.gov. We evaluated translation fidelity via a back-translation approach, using LLMs to convert TEL formulas back into natural language and measuring semantic similarity against source texts. The resulting semantic retention suggests that LLMs may offer a pathway for mapping informal protocols to computable logic, providing preliminary evidence toward scalable clinical trial emulation within the emerging "Symbolic Biomedicine" paradigm championed by the corresponding author.

</details>


### [25] [Ensemble Logic for Symbolic Representation of Sleep Medicine Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21331)
*Jiahao Fan,Xiaojin Li,Yan Huang,Xubing Hao,Licong Cui,Guo-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 研究将睡眠多导图评分指南中的叙述性规则形式化为理性集合逻辑（QEL）规范，以降低人工评分与软件实现中的解释差异，并提供可执行的标准化规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床评分指南虽为标准，但其自然语言描述可能存在多种合理解读，导致评分者之间的一致性不足，以及不同研究和软件系统在规则实现上的差异。

Method: 作者采用规则提取与编译流程，将指南中的可评分事件转换为基于连续有理数时间轴的QEL表达。QEL结合一阶量化与度量时序算子。研究识别出18个独特原子命题，并构建12项对应临床可评分事件的最终形式化规范；随后通过将规范回译为临床语言，并以嵌入余弦相似度和ROUGE-L评估语义及词汇保真度。

Result: 回译文本与原始规则具有较高语义相似度，嵌入余弦相似度为79.3，95%置信区间为79.0至79.7；但词汇重叠较低，ROUGE-L为18.3，95%置信区间为17.6至18.9。形式化过程还揭示了原规则中隐含的生理延迟和相互重叠的排除条件等潜在歧义。

Conclusion: QEL可将叙述性睡眠评分规则转化为严谨、可执行且可复用的逻辑规范，有望支持计算表型分析、跨数据集和软件的一致实现，以及开源睡眠多导图分析的标准化。其主要价值在于提升规则可验证性与可解释性；但摘要未报告与人工评分或既有软件实现进行端到端一致性验证的结果。

Abstract: The American Academy of Sleep Medicine (AASM) Manual is the clinical standard for polysomnography (PSG) scoring, but its narrative rules can admit multiple reasonable interpretations, contributing to inter-scorer variability and implementation differences across studies and software systems. We present a formal framework for translating sleep-scoring rules into Rational Ensemble Logic (QEL), a dense-time (i.e., a continuous, rational-valued timeline rather than discrete steps) formalism that combines first-order quantification with metric temporal operators. Using an extraction-and-compilation procedure, we identified 18 unique atomic propositions and derived 12 final specifications corresponding to clinically scoreable AASM events. Back-translation of QEL specifications into clinician-facing language retained high semantic fidelity to the original scoring narratives (embedding cosine similarity: 79.3, 95% CI: 79.0--79.7) despite low lexical overlap (ROUGE-L: 18.3, 95 CI: 17.6--18.9). Formalization also clarifies latent ambiguities, including implicit physiological latencies and overlapping exclusions. This framework yields executable, rigorous rule specifications for computational phenotyping, more consistent implementation across datasets, and standardized open-source PSG analysis. This work is a part of the "Symbolic Biomedicine" program championed by the corresponding author.

</details>
