### [26] [PhantomSeal: Proactive Deepfakes Defense with Identity/Context Protection and Forensic Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20564)
*Liangqin Ren,Zeyan Liu,Ye Wang,Yuxin Chen,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PhantomSeal是一种面向换脸伪造的主动防御方法：在图像发布前嵌入隐蔽的“伪装身份”特征，使后续换脸模型倾向生成该伪装身份而非目标用户身份，同时保留对伪造内容的取证追踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有换脸检测与溯源方法大多在伪造内容产生后进行识别，难以从源头阻止用户图像被滥用。主动防御方案通常仅保护身份或缺乏实际场景下的稳健性，因此需要一种兼顾身份保护、图像语境保护和事后取证的预防性机制。

Method: 论文提出PhantomSeal，通过一种图像伪装技术，将预先选定的身份以隐蔽标识的形式嵌入原始图像。该标识在视觉上尽量不显著，但会干预换脸模型的特征提取与生成过程，使生成结果更接近预设的伪装身份；同时，系统利用该身份特征对被篡改内容进行基于特征的司法分析与归因。作者在多种换脸架构和模型上评估其有效性与鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，PhantomSeal能够显著降低换脸攻击成功率，并维持较高的伪造内容识别能力。针对SimSwap，攻击成功率被降至0.30%；对于被操纵内容，其正确识别率达到97.97%。这些结果说明该方法在跨模型换脸防护和后续追踪方面具有较强效果。

Conclusion: PhantomSeal将预防性图像保护与取证溯源结合，为换脸滥用提供了从生成前干预到生成后识别的一体化方案。其核心贡献是利用可控身份伪装来误导换脸生成过程；但摘要未披露对图像感知质量、对自适应攻击者、不同真实图像分布及长期部署成本的具体评估，这些仍是判断实际适用性的关键。

Abstract: Deepfakes, especially face-swapping attacks, pose significant challenges to authenticity, security, and ethics across science, engineering, and society. While most existing detection/tracing approaches operate post hoc, proactive defenses that aim to intervene before deepfake generation remain limited in terms of real-world effectiveness. In this paper, we present PhantomSeal, the first proactive defense to simultaneously protect both the identity and the context of users' images from being used in face-swapping attacks, while supporting forensic tracing. We present a novel cloaking technique that embeds a selected identity as a stealthy identifier. This mechanism steers the deepfake generation process toward producing content that resembles the chosen cloak identity, thereby preventing successful face-swapping while enabling effective feature-based forensic analysis. The effectiveness and robustness of PhantomSeal is demonstrated in extensive experiments across different face-swapping architectures and models. For example, it reduces the attack success rate of SimSwap, an advanced deepfake model, to 0.30%, and correctly identifies 97.97% of manipulated content. Codes can be found at https://github.com/LiangqinRen/PhantomSeal

</details>


### [27] [AuthProbe: Specification-Driven, Multi-Identity Detection of Broken Object-Level Authorization in Recruitment API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20574)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出AuthProbe：一种开源黑盒HTTP API扫描器，利用OpenAPI规范和多个受控身份检测对象级授权缺陷，并可集成至持续集成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对象级授权缺陷难以通过传统Web应用防火墙或单一身份扫描发现，因为攻击请求与正常请求在字节层面可能完全一致。现有方法缺少跨身份、基于真实对象归属关系的自动化验证能力。

Method: AuthProbe依据OpenAPI规范生成测试请求，分别以多个受控身份发现其合法拥有的对象；随后以身份A访问身份B的对象，并将结果与对象真实拥有者的响应进行比对以确认越权读取。工具还遍历可预测标识符以检测枚举风险，同时报告缺失认证及对象存在性侧信道，并输出机器可读报告和基于严重度阈值的退出码。

Result: 在一个复现典型招聘API越权读取问题的合成系统中，AuthProbe检测到全部预置的跨身份读取缺陷；在加固版本中未产生误报。实验显示，其运行时间随待测对象数量线性增长。

Conclusion: AuthProbe将基于规范的接口测试、跨身份对象访问和响应真值比对结合起来，可有效发现传统单身份安全扫描难以识别的对象级授权问题，并具备自动化工程集成价值。

Abstract: Broken Object-Level Authorization (BOLA), also known as Insecure Direct Object Reference (IDOR), has topped the OWASP API Security ranking since 2019 and is the root cause of some of the largest exposures of applicant data in recruitment technology. The defining feature of this flaw class is that a malicious request is byte-for-byte indistinguishable from a legitimate one, which is precisely why web application firewalls and single identity scanners fail to catch it. We present AuthProbe, an open-source, black-box scanner that detects BOLA and IDOR in HTTP APIs by driving its tests from an OpenAPI specification and by acting under two or more identities that the operator controls. AuthProbe discovers, for each identity, the objects that identity legitimately owns, then attempts to read one identity's objects while authenticated as another and confirms a leak by comparing the response against a ground-truth fetch by the true owner. It also walks predictable identifiers to expose enumeration and reports missing authentication and existence oracles. The tool returns a severity-thresholded exit code and machine-readable reports so that it can gate a continuous integration build. On a synthetic recruitment API in which the McHire failure class is reproduced, AuthProbe detects every planted cross-identity read with no false positives on a hardened counterpart, and its running time grows linearly with the number of objects under test. AuthProbe is released under the Apache 2.0 license with an authorized-use guardrail.

</details>


### [28] [Deepfake News Detection: A Multimodal Framework Integrating LipNet, DeepSpeech and ResNET for Enhanced Audio-Visual Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20579)
*Ameena Khan,Muhammad Ahsan Aziz,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Naeem Akhter,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于识别伪造新闻视频的多模态检测框架，融合唇部运动、语音内容与人脸视觉特征，并通过集成分类器判断视频真实性；在FakeAVCeleb数据集上最高达到94%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能降低了高逼真度伪造视频与克隆语音的制作门槛，单一模态检测容易受到伪造质量、压缩失真和跨模态不一致性的影响。因此，研究旨在联合音频、口型和视觉线索，提高视频真实性检测的鲁棒性与实用性。

Method: 框架从三个维度提取特征：使用LipNet编码唇部运动，使用DeepSpeech2提取语音内容特征，使用BlazeFace定位人脸并由ResNet18生成面部视觉表征。随后，将各模态特征向量拼接为统一的视频表示，并分别采用随机森林、MLP和LSTM等机器学习与深度学习模型进行分类和集成判别。

Result: 在FakeAVCeleb数据集上的实验表明，采用增强音频特征时，该方法取得94%的准确率，并优于文中选取的现有多模态集成基线。结果显示音频增强和多模态特征融合对提升检测性能具有明显作用。

Conclusion: 该研究说明，联合建模语音、口型同步关系和面部视觉特征能够有效提升伪造视频检测能力。其主要贡献在于构建了由成熟预训练组件与多类分类器组成的多模态流程；但摘要未报告精确率、召回率、跨数据集泛化能力、消融实验及实际场景下的计算开销，仍需进一步验证方法的普适性。

Abstract: Deepfake news refers to AI-generated (or AI ma-nipulated) multimedia content intentionally generated to deceive audiences by manipulating the facial expressions, or speech while maintaining the realistic appearance. The rapid progress of generative AI has made the synthesis of highly realistic fake videos and cloned voices widely accessible, posing a serious threat to the authenticity of digital news media. This paper presents a multi-modal framework that discerns the authenticity of video content by jointly exploiting audio and visual cues, thereby addressing the challenge of detecting the deepfake videos. We proposed a framework that involves features extraction from lip movements, audio content and video frames. Lip movements and speech content are encoded using the LipNet and DeepSpeech2 models, while facial features are extracted by leveraging the use of BlazeFace and represented with ResNet18. The extracted feature vectors are concatenated into a holistic video representation and classified with an ensemble of machine learning and deep learning models, including Random Forest (RF), Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. Exten-sive experiments performed on the FakeAVCeleb dataset shows that the proposed approach attains an accuracy of 94% using augmented audio features, outperforming a state-of-the-art multi-modal ensemble baseline. The results confirm the robustness and practical potential of the proposed framework for deepfake news detection.

</details>


### [29] [Geometric Configurations of Perturbed Jailbreak Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20581)
*Lynn Delcon,Andres Algaba,Vincent Ginis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究字符串级扰动越狱提示在多种小型指令模型中的内部表征，发现所考察的表征空间未呈现可稳定区分行为的线性边界，仅少数下一词元与顺从性回答标签存在显著关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不断演化的提示扰动可能改变模型对原本失败输入的响应方式，因此作者希望检验模型内部表征中是否存在能够识别或预测顺从性回答的明确结构。

Method: 作者在Qwen-2.5与Llama-3.2/3.1多个小参数量指令模型上，分析两类表征：最后一层最后一个词元的嵌入，以及概率最高的50个下一词元构成的概率空间。随后比较不同扰动提示的聚类特征，并在以拒答为主的数据集中寻找可分离行为标签的超平面及与顺从标签显著相关的词元。

Result: 最后词元嵌入主要按照拼写和格式差异分离提示；前50下一词元概率空间近似一维，但聚类现象更复杂。在两个空间中，作者均未发现能稳定划分回答行为的超平面。显著关联仅出现在少数词元：Qwen-2.5-1.5B中的“Sure”，以及Llama模型中的“,”和“ĊĊ”。

Conclusion: 字符串扰动导致的回答差异并未在所测模型和所选表征空间中表现为简单、普适的线性可分行为信号。少数下一词元可作为局部关联指标，但不足以构成可靠的通用检测或预测机制。

Abstract: Perturbation techniques that turn unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones are continuously evolving, constituting a major security threat to LLM safety. In this paper, we investigate the internal representations of such string-level perturbed jailbreak inputs in the small weight models of the Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct families. We select two representation spaces: the last-layer-last-token embedding space and the top-50 next-token probability space. The former space separates prompts based on their spelling and format, while the latter space is effectively one-dimensional but appears more complex to cluster. Within our refusal-dominated answer set we find no behavioral hyperplane in either space. Only the next token "Sure" in the 1.5B Qwen model, and both tokens "," and "ĊĊ" in the 1$ Llama model, display a significant association with a compliant-labeled answer.

</details>


### [30] [Enhancing Attack Detection Capabilities in BACnet/IP Networks Using Machine-Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20686)
*Derek Manzella,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文面向BACnet/IP楼宇自动化网络的异常检测，改造Zeek解析器、构建带逐包标签的仿真测试床，并评估五种无监督模型；One-Class SVM总体最佳，平均F1为0.864。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACnet/IP承担暖通空调等关键楼宇功能，但现有防御工具和针对BACnet协议的标注数据集有限，导致机器学习检测方法难以开发、训练和公平评估。

Method: 研究将CISA的Zeek BACnet解析器改造成统一的逐包日志格式，以简化特征工程；使用bacpypes3构建具有物理行为、控制器时序逻辑和逐包攻击标签的小型商业HVAC仿真环境；基于正常流量及六类异常行为流量，比较五种无监督异常检测模型。

Result: One-Class SVM在各类场景中表现最优，平均F1分数为0.864。对高流量异常及扫描型行为的检测效果尤佳，F1超过0.99；对属性篡改和虚假数据注入等低隐蔽、低流量异常的检测较弱，F1约为0.77。

Conclusion: 统一协议日志与具备行为真实性的仿真标注数据可有效支持BACnet/IP异常检测研究。无监督模型对流量模式显著偏离正常状态的异常尤其有效，但对隐蔽的语义级篡改仍存在明显局限，未来应结合过程状态、控制逻辑和更细粒度时序特征提升检测能力。

Abstract: Building Automation Systems (BAS) manage critical building functions using protocols such as BACnet/IP, yet defenders have limited tooling and few labeled datasets for detecting BACnet-specific attacks. This work addresses these gaps through three contributions. First, CISA's Zeek BACnet parser is modified to produce a unified per-packet log, simplifying feature engineering for machine-learning (ML) pipelines. Second, a simulated BACnet/IP testbed is developed using bacpypes3 to model a small commercial HVAC system with physics-based device behavior, schedule-aware controller logic, and per-packet attack labeling. Third, five unsupervised anomaly detection models are evaluated using baseline traffic and six BACnet attack types, including denial of service, reconnaissance, property tampering, and false data injection. Results show that One-Class SVM achieved the strongest overall performance, with an average F1 score of 0.864 across all attacks and F1 scores above 0.99 for high-volume denial-of-service and reconnaissance attacks. Detection is much stronger for high-volume attacks, such as DoS attacks and reconnaissance, than stealthier techniques such as tampering and false data injection, which scored around 77%.

</details>


### [31] [Buzz to Boom: Detecting Message Progression Vulnerabilities in Electron Applications via Segmented Directed Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20698)
*Jianjia Yu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yu Sun,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出Proton，一种面向Electron跨进程消息传递漏洞的分段定向模糊测试框架，用于发现消息在不同权限进程间传播并最终触达高权限接口的消息传播漏洞（MPV）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Electron安全研究主要关注不安全配置和恶意DOM内容，难以覆盖需要多阶段、跨进程消息传递才能触发的漏洞链。此类漏洞通常要求先在一个进程中通过消息获得代码执行能力，再构造后续消息影响另一高权限进程，因此端到端测试的路径探索困难较大。

Method: Proton以进程间消息边界为界，将端到端模糊测试拆分为多个按进程执行的测试片段。每个片段的目标是到达当前进程中的危险接口，或将测试载荷通过消息传递至下一进程；对于后者，输出消息将作为下一片段的语料种子。最后，框架综合各进程中发现的崩溃输入，以验证完整的跨进程利用链。

Result: 作者在589个真实Electron应用上评估Proton，发现23个此前未知的MPV，其中22个可导致操作系统命令执行。受影响项目包含GitHub星标数超过5万的项目。披露后，研究获得13项确认、11项修复和11个CVE，并获得一项漏洞奖励。

Conclusion: Proton表明，按消息传递边界进行分段定向模糊测试能够有效解决Electron多进程漏洞链的探索问题，并发现传统配置检查或DOM分析难以检测的高影响跨进程安全缺陷。

Abstract: Electron is a popular framework for building cross-platform desktop applications using web technologies. Such applications consist of multiple processes with different privilege levels that communicate via message passing. When inter-process messages carry attacker-controlled inputs, they can propagate across processes and reach privileged APIs, e.g., command execution. Such a message propagation behavior is characterized as Message Progression Vulnerabilities (MPVs). The exploitation of MPVs is challenging because it often requires multiple steps, e.g., first arbitrary code execution in one process via message passing, and then command injection in another process using another message crafted in the first process. To our knowledge, existing works on Electron security only study unsafe configurations and malicious Document Object Model (DOM) content, i.e., they cannot detect or exploit these vulnerabilities that need to be triggered by complex cross-process exploits via message passing. We present Proton, a segmented directed fuzzing framework for detecting MPVs. Our key insight is to decompose end-to-end fuzzing into per-process segments along message-passing boundaries, where the goals of fuzzing each segment are either: (i) reaching a sink in the current process or (ii) propagating the payload to the next process, to enable the exploration of another process. In the second case, the messages seed the corpus of the next segment. Finally, Proton synthesizes crash inputs from each process to validate end-to-end exploits. We evaluate Proton against 589 real-world Electron applications, resulting in 23 zero-day MPVs. Among them, 22 lead to OS command execution, including projects with over 50k GitHub stars. We responsibly disclosed all findings. To date, we have received 13 acknowledgments, 11 fixes, and 11 CVEs, including a bug bounty from Vercel.

</details>


### [32] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Symbolic Security Protocol Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20712)
*Paolo Modesti,Syed Ahmed,Ioannis Sfyrakis,Derek Enodolomwanyi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究比较GPT与DeepSeek在安全协议验证任务上的表现，结果显示其无法替代ProVerif、OFMC等形式化工具；LLM最多可作为预筛选手段，尤其在保密性目标上具有一定价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全协议验证通常依赖形式化方法，但其建模与使用成本较高。研究旨在评估LLM能否对经混淆的AnB/AnBx协议进行安全目标判断，并系统考察聊天模式与推理模式在准确性、稳定性及置信度校准方面的差异。

Method: 作者对130个混淆协议、388项安全目标进行测试，每种模型配置运行三次，并以ProVerif和OFMC的结论作为参照。评估指标包括召回率、精确率、F1值、跨运行结论一致性，以及模型自报置信度与实际正确性的相关性；同时区分保密性、注入式一致性和非注入式一致性等目标类型。

Result: 聊天模型的召回率为69%至81%，但精确率低于31%，表现为大量误报。推理模型提高了精确率：GPT达到66.5%，DeepSeek达到45.4%，但只能检出略多于一半的攻击。DeepSeek的聊天与推理模式基于同一底层模型，推理使其精确率从27.2%提升至45.4%，较能说明推理机制的贡献。认证目标最困难，推理模型对两类一致性攻击的检出率均明显低于一半，而聊天模型虽更常报警但精确率较低。保密性任务例外，推理模式的F1最高可达95.7%。运行稳定性方面，GPT在89.7%的目标上结论一致，DeepSeek仅为74.0%；两者自报置信度普遍偏高，且与正确性基本无相关性。

Conclusion: LLM在该基准上未达到形式化验证工具的可靠性，主要问题是认证性质分析不足、结论跨运行不稳定，以及置信度无法反映真实性能。推理模式能在一定程度上减少误报，但会牺牲攻击检出率。实际应用中，LLM适合辅助初步筛查或提示潜在问题，不应作为安全协议验证的最终判定依据。

Abstract: Security protocol verification relies on formal tools such as ProVerif and OFMC. This study evaluates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) can perform comparable analysis. We test GPT and DeepSeek in chat and reasoning modes over three runs on 130 obfuscated AnB/AnBx protocols covering 388 security goals, scored against ProVerif and OFMC. Chat models reach 69 to 81% recall at precision below 31%. Reasoning models reverse this trade-off, reaching 66.5% precision for GPT and 45.4% for DeepSeek, but detect just over half the attacks. DeepSeek's two modes share one underlying model, so the comparison isolates reasoning itself, which raises precision from 27.2% to 45.4%. The GPT contrast spans a model-version change and is only suggestive. All models perform worst on authentication goals: reasoning models detect well under half of injective and non-injective agreement attacks, whereas chat models over-flag them at low precision. Confidentiality is the exception, with F1 up to 95.7% in reasoning mode. Verdicts are unstable across runs, identical on 89.7% of goals for GPT but 74.0% for DeepSeek. Self-reported confidence is uniformly high yet shows no meaningful correlation with correctness. On this benchmark LLMs do not match formal verification, but may serve, at best, as pre-screening filters.

</details>


### [33] [Security Vulnerability Patterns in AI-Generated Code: A Cross-Model Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20713)
*Shanna M. Kahn,John D. Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，多个主流大语言模型生成的自动化脚本均存在可被利用的安全缺陷，且不同模型之间的总体风险差异较小；核心问题在于未经审查部署自动化代码，而非某一特定模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非专业用户可借助大语言模型快速生成脚本并将其引入组织工作流，但这类代码通常缺乏正式安全审查。研究旨在量化此类脚本的安全风险，并判断风险是否集中于特定模型。

Method: 研究者针对三个自动化领域向ChatGPT、Microsoft Copilot和Google Gemini提交相同提示词并收集代码；随后使用Claude Code进行标准化缺陷审查。识别出的缺陷采用CVSS v3.1评分，并映射至OWASP Top 10:2021及MITRE ATT&CK框架。

Result: 所有脚本均含有可被利用的缺陷。共识别17类缺陷，其中9类同时出现在三个模型生成的代码中，14类至少出现在两个模型中。各平台的加权CVSS评分差异不足10%，表明安全风险在不同模型间较为一致。

Conclusion: 风险主要由自动化任务类型及缺乏安全审查所驱动，而非由单一模型造成。组织不应仅关注选择何种代码生成工具，而应建立对大语言模型生成自动化代码的部署前审查、测试和安全治理机制。

Abstract: LLM-based coding tools enable non-expert users to generate routine automation scripts that may enter enterprise workflows without meaningful security review. This study examines that risk directly. Code was collected from ChatGPT, Microsoft Copilot, and Google Gemini using identical prompts across three automation domains. Claude Code performed a standardized vulnerability review. Each identified vulnerability was scored using CVSS v3.1 and mapped to the OWASP Top 10:2021 and the MITRE ATT&CK frameworks. Every script contained exploitable vulnerabilities. Nine of the 17 identified vulnerability classes appeared in code from all three models, while 14 of the 17 vulnerability classes appeared in at least two models. The weighted CVSS scores across platforms differed by less than 10%. The risk is not tied to any particular model but rather to the task category. Organizations should therefore ask not which tool to trust, but instead whether LLM-generated automation code should be deployed without review.

</details>


### [34] [IssueTrojanBench: Benchmarking AI Coding Agents Against Malicious Issue Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20759)
*Ankur Singh,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了主流AI编程代理面对恶意问题请求时的安全性，发现现有部署中的代理与模型防护存在显著缺口：IssueTrojanBench中66.5%的恶意请求可穿透全部防线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理具备生成、修改和执行代码以及调用本地工具的能力，因此同时继承了语言模型的提示注入、训练数据污染和后门风险，以及代理架构带来的工具滥用、数据泄露和环境持久化风险。论文旨在量化现实开发场景下恶意问题请求对该类系统的威胁。

Method: 作者构建IssueTrojanBench基准，将恶意指令嵌入问题请求中，并设计四类攻击类别、六种投递载体（如PDF和问题评论）及多种扰动方式。实验测试Cursor、Claude Code和Codex Desktop三类编程代理，并比较GPT-5.3 Codex、GPT-5.4与Sonnet 4.6模型在代理级和模型级防护下的表现。

Result: 实验显示，66.5%的恶意问题请求能够绕过代理级与模型级的全部防护。拒绝恶意请求的主要来源是底层语言模型，而非代理框架；GPT系列模型整体更易受影响，Sonnet 4.6则对高影响操作表现出更具选择性和风险意识的拦截能力。现有代理层防御对提升整体安全性的增益有限。

Conclusion: 当前AI编程代理的安全边界不足，尤其不能依赖代理框架独立阻断恶意任务。论文建议同时强化模型层的风险识别与拒绝能力，以及代理层对指令来源、外部文件、工具调用和高风险操作的隔离、验证与审计机制。

Abstract: AI coding agents powered by LLMs are increasingly integrated into real-world software development, where they generate, edit, and execute code with autonomous access to local files and tools. Coding agents inherit security risks from both the LLM backbone, where adversarial prompts, poisoned training data, and backdoor triggers can cause models to emit insecure or attacker-chosen code, and their agentic architecture, where tool-using autonomy enables induced misuse of external APIs, data exfiltration, and persistent compromise of development environments. This paper presents a systematic evaluation of malicious issue requests against state-of-the-art coding agents (Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex Desktop), powered by two major model families (OpenAI GPT-5.3 Codex/GPT-5.4 and Anthropic Sonnet 4.6). Our novel benchmark IssueTrojanBench contains malicious issues that are constructed based on four novel attack categories (i.e., embedded as malicious instructions in issues), six delivery vectors (e.g., PDF, or issue comment), and further augmented by perturbations. Our results reveal critical vulnerabilities in the as-deployed modern coding agents, i.e., 66.5% of the malicious issues from IssueTrojanBench penetrate all the guardrails (agent- and LLM-level) of coding agents. Our further analysis shows that rejection is almost entirely from LLMs rather than the agent frameworks, with GPT models broadly vulnerable and Sonnet 4.6 exhibiting more selective, risk-aware blocking of high-impact actions. Our evaluation also highlights that the current agent-level defense strategy offers limited additional protection for coding agents. Our findings highlight the urgent need for stronger agent- and model-level safety mechanisms to protect AI coding agents.

</details>


### [35] [Leaky Language Models: Stealing Architecture and Inference Optimizations via Per-Token Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20723)
*Sadegh Majidi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Kazem Taram*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出LeakyLMs：一种仅利用远程API返回的逐token生成时延，推断生产级语言模型部署优化策略与部分模型架构参数的侧信道攻击框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源模型提供方通常不公开具体架构、推理优化与部署配置，但这些信息具有商业价值，并可能暴露系统实现层面的侧信道风险。现有方法较少证明仅凭生成时延即可系统恢复这些细节。

Method: 方法包含两类攻击：（1）通过分析时延模式识别推理优化和部署策略，例如检测是否采用推测解码，并估计草稿模型的上下文窗口；（2）建立现代NVIDIA GPU上token生成延迟与模型配置、硬件参数之间的精细性能模型，再在候选架构空间中搜索，以推断Transformer层数、隐藏维度和注意力头数。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可识别推测解码及其草稿模型上下文长度；论文报告某商业模型采用推测解码，草稿上下文窗口约为128K。对于Llama系列模型，接近真实的架构配置在超过90%的实验中进入前10个候选结果。

Conclusion: Token生成时延可构成有效的远程侧信道，使攻击者在无需访问模型权重、日志或内部系统的情况下推断部署与架构信息。论文提示模型服务商应将时延特征视为信息泄露面，并考虑降低可观测时延模式、引入扰动或采用更一致的服务策略。

Abstract: This work presents LeakyLMs, a set of attacks that leak proprietary model, architecture, and deployment information from production language models. LeakyLMs is the first to demonstrate that key model and deployment details can be inferred using only token generation timing, even when interacting through remote APIs. LeakyLMs introduces two core attacks. The first attack targets inference optimizations and deployment strategies. For example, our attack detects whether a provider uses speculative decoding, a widely deployed inference-time optimization, and further identifies the context length of the draft model used in the pipeline. Our measurements show that Google Gemini Flash 2.5 uses speculative decoding with a draft context window of approximately 128K tokens. The second attack recovers key architectural properties, including the number of transformer layers, hidden dimension size, and number of attention heads. To achieve this, LeakyLMs builds a detailed and accurate model of token-generation timing on modern NVIDIA GPUs, characterizing how latency scales with model configuration and hardware parameters. The attack then performs a search over the architecture space using this timing model. In experiments with Llama models, the near-correct architectural configuration appears in the top-10 guesses more than 90% of the time.

</details>


### [36] [GPE: Evaluating Robust Evidence Aggregation for Fact Verification under Controllable GEO-Style Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20730)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Xiaomei Yuan,Pengtao Kou,Jiamou Liu,Zhen Li,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出GPE基准与评测框架，用于衡量大语言模型在受操纵检索证据环境下进行事实核验时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强模型依赖外部搜索结果，而生成式引擎优化可提高特定内容被检索、引用和采纳的概率。现有事实核验基准通常仅在干净证据条件下评估，无法系统检验模型面对受污染证据时的可靠性。

Method: 作者构建GPE，包括覆盖多个领域的事实核验基准，以及可控制证据来源和污染比例的评测框架；随后在多种核验方法与不同证据污染策略下开展对比实验。

Result: 实验显示，随着受污染证据的引入，多个核验方法的鲁棒性会显著下降，同时呈现准确性、鲁棒性与计算或检索效率之间的权衡；这些问题在仅使用干净数据的评测中难以发现。

Conclusion: GPE为评估检索增强事实核验系统在对抗性证据环境中的表现提供了受控工具。论文表明，事实核验评测应纳入证据操纵场景，而不能仅依赖传统的干净环境测试。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly use search tools to retrieve up-to-date information, introducing a new attack surface in which retrieved documents can be manipulated. This risk is amplified by the development of generative engine optimization, which can make selected content more likely to be retrieved, cited, and adopted by models. Existing fact-verification benchmarks and evaluation frameworks do not provide the controlled evidence environments needed to assess robustness against GEO poisoning. We therefore propose GPE, which consists of a multi-domain fact-verification benchmark and an evaluation framework for controlling evidence sources and poisoning ratios. Experiments across multiple verification methods and poisoning attacks demonstrate that GPE exposes robustness degradation and efficiency trade-offs that cannot be observed through clean evaluation alone, confirming the need to evaluate fact verification under adversarial evidence environments.

</details>


### [37] [Classical Acceptance Is Not Hybrid Authentication: Measuring X.509 Verifier Semantics in Post-Quantum Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20800)
*Taesung Kim,Boheung Chung,Keonwoo Kim,Yousung Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出：混合X.509证书即使同时携带传统密码凭证与后量子凭证，许多验证器仍可能仅依据传统证书路径接受，而未让后量子证据实际影响验证结论，形成“经典验证成功被静默提升为混合验证成功”的结构性降级风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着后量子迁移，混合证书旨在兼容现有PKI并同时提供传统与后量子安全性。然而，若后量子凭证被放置于传统路径验证可忽略的位置，系统表面上支持混合证书，实际却无法证明接受结果受后量子证据支撑。论文关注规范是否明确要求这种绑定及其在真实验证实现中的执行情况。

Method: 作者构建了源自规范的验证器模型，并给出可执行、策略参数化的参考契约，规定验证器在报告“混合验证”前必须识别、验证、使后量子证据成为决策结果的一部分，并执行相应状态检查。实验覆盖8个路径验证栈、7个独立代码库、9种验证模式及6类证书方案；同时通过后量子绑定凭证已撤销而传统证书仍有效的生命周期失同步场景，测试实际降级是否可被触发。

Result: 在要求混合验证的策略下，几乎所有能够解析可分离式混合证书的验证栈都会仅沿传统路径接受，且后量子证据不具有结果决定性。一个强制执行模式因尚未形成可互操作配置的签名输入编码而损害互操作性。即使部分实现能够验证后量子签名，默认情况下仍不强制其与传统证书的绑定及其对最终结论的影响。生命周期失同步实验进一步表明：后量子凭证被撤销后，只要传统证书有效，默认验证路径仍会接受。

Conclusion: 问题核心不是缺少后量子密码原语支持，而是验证语义与标准要求存在结构性缺口：成功建立绑定不等于完成混合认证。混合证书标准与实现应明确区分传统路径有效和混合验证有效，并要求后量子凭证的有效性、绑定关系及撤销状态均纳入接受判定；参考契约可作为实现与合规测试的基线。

Abstract: A relying party validating a hybrid X.509 certificate --- carrying both a classical and a post-quantum credential --- must distinguish whether its accepting judgment rests on the post-quantum evidence or only on the classical path. To preserve compatibility, the separable designs place that evidence where classical path validation may ignore it. A verifier can then validate the classical path and accept while the post-quantum evidence never bears on the decision --- a valid classical result silently promoted to a hybrid conclusion it did not establish. We measure this across eight path-validation stacks (seven independent codebases), in nine validation modes, over six certificate schemes. Under a hybrid-required policy, nearly every stack parsing a separable hybrid certificate accepts on the classical path without making the post-quantum evidence outcome-bearing; one enforcing mode instead fractures interoperability over a signature-input encoding not yet interoperably profiled; and stacks that verify post-quantum signatures still do not enforce the binding by default: the gap is structural, not explained by missing primitive capability alone. Under lifecycle desynchronization the downgrade is realized: when a bound post-quantum credential is revoked while the classical certificate stays valid, the default path still accepts, because the bound credential lies outside the decision's scope. Binding success is not authentication success. We contribute a specification-derived verifier model and an executable, policy-parametric reference contract --- what a verifier must recognize, verify, make outcome-bearing, and check before reporting a validation as hybrid --- with a diagnosis of why standards do not require it.

</details>


### [38] [Which Model Is Actually Serving You? IRIS: Budgeted Black-Box Auditing of Model Substitution and Routing Dilution in LLM Gateways](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20860)
*Yuewei Zhang,Zhi-Hai Zhang,Hanzhang Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IRIS是一种仅依赖模型生成文本的黑盒审计方法，用于识别商业大模型网关是否将宣称的后端替换为其他模型，或仅按一定比例将请求路由至较低成本模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒审计通常依赖对数概率、词元排名、参考样本等特权信息，或需要针对目标模型设计探针；同时多固定查询预算并只输出二元结论，难以同时处理全量替换、部分稀释、后端归因和路由比例估计。

Method: IRIS要求端点生成随机数字或字符串，并基于输出分布构建后端指纹。方法先以低成本试验拟合查询次数与误差之间的指数衰减关系，随后在发起可疑端点查询前冻结所需预算。审计过程支持检测全量模型替换与比例为ε的混合路由、识别实际服务后端、估计ε，并通过自适应查询分配提升给定预算下的检测效率。

Result: 在Qwen3同系列模型实验中，IRIS的后端验证AUROC达到0.99，且查询增加会提高归因精度。在商业模型库实验中，对于满足间隔条件的模型对，检测ε=0.3的稀释行为取得平均0.85检验功效和0.017假阳性率；对已登记的混入模型，ε估计误差可控制在0.04以内。跨服务商实测中，15对名义相同模型的服务中有14对被识别出量化或内核差异；自适应分配将等预算条件下的目标命中率从73%提升至87%。

Conclusion: IRIS将文本级指纹、替换检测、后端归因、混合比例估计和预算自适应整合为统一的黑盒审计框架，在不访问模型内部信号的条件下仍具备较强检测能力。其有效性依赖于候选后端之间存在可稳定观测的输出分布差异；对于未登记的混入模型、模型参数设置差异及对抗性网关，仍需结合实验中的鲁棒性与误报控制结果审慎解释。

Abstract: Commercial LLM gateways mediate access to hosted models, but the served backend may not match the advertised one: it may substitute a cheaper model on every request or route only a fraction $ε$ of requests to it. Prior black-box auditors often need a privileged signal (log-probabilities, token ranks, or reference samples) or a target-specific probe, fix the query budget in advance, and return a yes/no verdict. We present $\mathrm{IRIS}$, an audit that needs only the returned text: it asks endpoints to generate random numbers or strings, fingerprints the backend, and is the first to combine, in one text-only audit, detection of whole-stream substitution and fractional dilution, attribution of the served backend, routing-fraction ($ε$) estimation, and a query budget it sizes itself. A cheap pilot fits the exponential query-error decay and freezes that budget before any suspect query is issued. On an intra-family Qwen3 ladder $\mathrm{IRIS}$ verifies the backend at $0.99$ AUROC and sharpens attribution as queries accumulate; across a commercial OpenRouter library it catches $ε{=}0.3$ dilution on margin-qualified pairs at $0.85$ mean power ($0.017$ false-positive rate) and recovers $ε$ to within $0.04$ for enrolled diluents; and a live cross-provider audit flags $14$ of $15$ same-model provider pairs by genuine quantization and kernel deviations, corroborated on third-party MET traces. Against comparable black-box auditors, $\mathrm{IRIS}$ matches or beats detection on shared tasks, and adaptive allocation lifts the matched-budget target-hit rate from $73$% to $87$%. Further experiments cover adversarial gateways, knob identifiability, unseen diluents, and false-positive control.

</details>


### [39] [Toward cryptographically verifiable authorization for autonomous AI agents: A security hypothesis, preliminary formal model, and proof-of-concept implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21325)
*M. Llambí-Morillas,D. Fernández-Fernández*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“密码学可验证智能体授权”（CVA）框架，将智能体主体、具体授权请求、执行上下文及策略满足性绑定为可密码学验证的关系，并以零知识证明原型展示其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认证与授权机制能够确认身份并委派权限，但难以提供可验证证据，证明某一智能体在特定运行上下文中发出的具体请求确实符合适用策略。随着自主智能体调用工具和访问受保护资源，这一缺口更加突出。

Method: 作者形式化定义关系R_CVA，联合描述主体、请求、上下文与策略合规性；提出授权健全性、主体绑定、请求绑定、策略绑定和抗重放等候选安全性质；基于Groth16 zk-SNARK构建可执行的零知识证明概念验证，实现模型的部分要素；并将身份绑定、授权请求绑定和运行时执行绑定之间的结构性分离形式化为开放问题。

Result: 论文给出了CVA的初步形式化抽象、紧凑的安全性质集合及一个基于Groth16的零知识原型。其核心发现是：仅证明身份或策略授权不足以保证实际运行中的具体请求与执行行为受到一致且可验证的约束，三类绑定之间仍存在关键断层。

Conclusion: CVA为智能体授权提供了从传统访问控制向可验证授权证据演进的研究方向。该工作目前主要是概念与原型验证，尚需解决运行时执行绑定、策略表达能力、系统集成开销及端到端安全证明等问题。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents increasingly execute actions, invoke tools, and operate on protected resources with limited human oversight. Existing authentication and authorization mechanisms establish identity and delegate authority, but do not inherently provide cryptographic evidence that a concrete request issued by a specific agent satisfies the applicable policy in a specific execution context. This paper hypothesizes that agent authorization can be formalized as a cryptographically verifiable relation, denoted $R_{CVA}$, that jointly binds an agent principal, a concrete authorization request, an execution context, and the satisfaction of an applicable policy, while selectively preserving the confidentiality of private authorization attributes. We introduce a preliminary formal abstraction for Cryptographically Verifiable Agent Authorization (CVA), define a compact set of candidate security properties including authorization soundness, principal binding, request binding, policy binding, and replay resistance, and provide an executable zero-knowledge proof of concept that instantiates selected elements of the model over a Groth16 zk-SNARK construction. We further identify and formalize the structural separation among identity binding, authorization-request binding, and runtime execution binding as a central open problem in the design of secure agentic systems (a distinction {not explicitly addressed by} current agentic security frameworks) and present a falsifiable research agenda for its resolution.

</details>


### [40] [Themis Consensus Extension v1: MEV Mitigation by Randomized Delayed Execution and Intent-Hiding Transactions in Application-Specific Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21406)
*Shoeb Siddiqui,Mateusz Nowakowski,Stanislav Vozarik,Gleb Urvanov,Peter Kris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文分析并规范化了Themis Consensus Extension v1，一种用于缓解区块链交易排序型价值提取与选择性拒绝型价值提取的共识扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块生产者可通过选择、插入、排除或重排待处理交易获取额外收益。该工作旨在将排序操纵与交易拒绝两类问题分离，并在明确威胁模型和系统假设下限制验证者的单边操纵能力。

Method: 对于排序型价值提取，方案将交易承诺与执行分配给相邻生产者：前者提交交易集合，后者依据公开可验证、确定性且事先不可预测的随机种子，对交易进行保持依赖关系的置换后执行。对于选择性拒绝，用户将交易加密给指定构建者和执行者；构建者仅去除外层并提交内部密文，执行者在承诺后解密并执行。论文还讨论了概率性提取、垃圾交易、依赖交易、解密活性、会话边界、完全拒绝及阈值联盟，并记录了从Aura/Substrate实现迁移至BABE、sr25519/VRF随机种子路径的工程细节。

Result: 在验证者自利但不串谋、攻击者能力低于底层共识容错阈值、密码学安全且角色行为可追责的条件下，该机制可限制承诺后的单方排序控制，并向中继者和构建者隐藏交易意图。实现采用延迟执行、Fisher-Yates洗牌及Xoshiro256++随机数生成。

Conclusion: Themis能够在有限假设下缓解部分交易排序操纵和选择性拒绝问题，但不保证发送或接收顺序公平性、完全抗审查性、构建者与执行者串谋安全性，也不提供单笔交易价格保证。论文的主要贡献是保留原始设计，同时将安全与公平性声明严格收缩至可验证的前提条件。

Abstract: Maximal extractable value (MEV) arises when privileged participants select, exclude, insert, or reorder pending transactions for private gain. We specify and analyze the Themis Consensus Extension v1, first published by Mangata in 2021. The design separates value extraction by reordering (VER) from value extraction by denial (VED). For VER, block construction and execution occur across consecutive producers: one producer commits a transaction set, and the next derives a publicly verifiable, deterministic, previously un- predictable seed and executes a seed-determined, dependency-preserving permutation. For selective VED, a user may encrypt a transaction for a designated builder and executor. The builder removes an outer layer and commits the opaque inner ciphertext; the executor reveals and executes the plaintext only after commitment. Under selfish but non-colluding validators, an adversary below the underlying consensus fault threshold, secure cryptography, and accountable role performance, the construction limits unilateral post-commit ordering control and hides transaction intent from relays and the builder. It does not provide send-order or receive-order fairness, complete censorship resistance, resistance to builder-executor collusion, or per-transaction price guarantees. We analyze probabilistic extraction, spam, dependent transactions, decryption liveness, session boundaries, total denial, and threshold coalitions. We also document the initial Aura-based Substrate implementation and its subsequent transition to a BABE-based sr25519/VRF seed path, together with delayed execution, Fisher-Yates shuffling, and Xoshiro256++. The result preserves the original proposal while narrowing its claims to explicit assumptions.

</details>


### [41] [Where You Tap Matters: A Probe-and-Model Benchmark for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21564)
*Gabriele Oligeri,Savio Sciancalepore,Ingrid Huso,Fatima Al-Mousawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了BPSK接收机链路不同采样点对开放集、重构误差式射频指纹识别性能的影响，发现采样点选择比自编码器结构复杂度更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对RFFI样本应在接收机链路的何处采集缺乏一致结论。载波恢复、增益归一化、脉冲成形和定时恢复等处理既可能降低同一发射机样本的波动，也可能抑制用于区分设备的硬件特征，因此需要在真实数据上进行系统比较。

Method: 研究在标准BPSK接收机链路的五个探测点采集数据，采用基于重构误差的开放集RFFI框架评估识别性能。为检验模型选择的影响，作者在固定预处理流程和均方误差评分规则的受控条件下，对多个由大语言模型设计的自编码器架构进行了基准测试。

Result: 结果表明，RFFI性能高度依赖采样探测点。定时恢复后的信号，以及较弱程度上的载波恢复后信号，可实现较低误接受水平，且分布内与分布外样本的重叠有限；其他处理阶段通常需要将误接受率提高到0.1以上，才能达到0.9的真接受率。不同自编码器架构均复现了这种探测点依赖性，但在选定工作点上未优于基线模型，且通常增加训练时间。

Conclusion: 对于基于重构误差的开放集RFFI，接收机链路中的采样位置是主导性能的因素，应优先于增加自编码器复杂度进行优化。定时恢复相关采样点尤其适合实现较低误接受率的发射机识别。

Abstract: Radio Frequency Fingerprint Identification (RFFI) enables transmitter identification at the physical layer by learning device-specific impairments from received signals, yet the literature is inconsistent about where in the receiver chain those samples should be collected. Since distinct transformations are applied to the signal by the different receiver operations, i.e., carrier recovery, gain normalization, pulse shaping, and timing recovery, they can either tighten within-transmitter variability or suppress the features RFFI requires for classification. We present a systematic real-world evaluation of open-set, reconstruction-error RFFI using data collected at five probe points along a standard BPSK receiver chain. Our results show that RFFI is strongly probe-dependent: timing recovery and, to a lesser extent, carrier recovery enable low false-acceptance operation with limited in-distribution-out-of-distribution overlap, whereas other stages often require a false-acceptance ratio above 0.1 to achieve a true-acceptance ratio of 0.9. To test the validity of our findings across model selection, we benchmark several LLM-designed autoencoders using a controlled pipeline that holds preprocessing and MSE scoring fixed. These architectures confirm that RFFI is probe-dependent. Moreover, they do not outperform the baseline at the chosen operating point and typically increase training time. Overall, probe selection dominates reconstruction-based open-set RFFI performance, more than the autoencoder complexity.

</details>

### [42] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AINTMA，一种面向云原生软件质量管理的多智能体系统，通过测试发现、风险评估、强化学习排序、执行编排、生成式质量分析和云安全监控六类智能体，实现测试流程的自动化协同与自适应决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测试管理难以适应分布式云环境中的大规模、动态和多租户软件交付需求，尤其在测试优先级确定、缺陷风险预测、执行编排及质量信息解释方面依赖人工决策。作者旨在构建具备自主协作能力、可扩展性和安全通信机制的质量智能平台。

Method: AINTMA采用云原生微服务架构和安全多智能体通信框架。强化学习排序智能体将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，基于覆盖36个月历史执行记录的47项特征学习上下文策略；生成式质量智能体使用大语言模型生成质量叙述、缺陷风险摘要及测试建议；系统通过零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT认证、加密消息传递和多租户隔离保障通信安全。实验在12个异构软件项目中持续18个月进行。

Result: 相较随机排序，系统测试优先级指标APFD达到88.4%，高于随机策略的51.2%和最佳商业基线的82.1%；测试周期缩短43%；缺陷逃逸率由8.3%降至2.1%；投资回报率为340%，回收期为9个月。系统可处理5万以上测试用例，响应时间低于400毫秒；生成式质量模块的开发者实用性评分为4.3/5.0。

Conclusion: AINTMA表明，多智能体协同、强化学习测试排序与生成式质量分析的结合，能够显著提升云规模企业的软件质量管理效率和测试效果。其结果支持将自主智能体架构用于测试管理，但摘要未说明各项目的具体分布、统计显著性、消融实验及对比基线配置，因而泛化能力和各模块的独立贡献仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [43] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了水印技术对医疗大语言模型与视觉语言模型临床推理能力的影响，发现通用基准往往掩盖水印导致的医学文本质量下降与潜在风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正进入临床工作流，水印可用于追踪模型输出来源，但医疗场景对术语准确性、推理完整性和影像发现描述极为敏感。现有水印研究主要依赖通用评测，难以揭示细微词元扰动在医学任务中的实际后果。

Method: 研究评测了5种水印方案，覆盖11个大语言模型和7个视觉语言模型，并在单模态及多模态临床推理任务上进行比较。同时，作者构建了经人类医学专家验证的审计流程，系统检查医学推理质量、术语精确性以及水印诱发的幻觉问题。

Result: 水印会在多个方面显著降低医疗任务表现，包括词汇或术语损坏、生成虚构医学术语，以及对医学图像发现的错误归因、遗漏和放大。仅使用聚合指标或非医学专用基准时，这些具有临床意义的失败模式可能被系统性忽略。

Conclusion: 医学领域部署带水印模型前，必须开展面向领域的细粒度评估。通用水印基准不足以保证医疗输出的可靠性；评估体系应重点纳入医学术语准确性、临床推理质量、幻觉及多模态发现保真度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [44] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一款用于识别标题党内容的人工智能浏览器扩展，结合文本嵌入、语言学特征和自定义“诱导性”评分，以在用户访问文章前后提供预警、概率评分、可解释依据及内容摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 误导性标题会诱导用户点击，降低信息获取效率。传统标题党检测通常仅基于标题文本，且缺少面向终端用户的实时预警、解释与文章内容概览能力。

Method: 系统比较了经典文本向量化方法与大语言模型嵌入表示，并构建混合特征架构：Transformer类嵌入、语言学启发式特征及自定义“baitness”评分共同输入XGBoost分类器。浏览器扩展可在点击前提示风险，在打开文章后输出标题党概率，并根据所分析指标解释预测结果；同时生成一至两句的文章摘要作为“剧透”。

Result: 在公开组合数据集上，所开发的XGBoost模型取得91%的F1分数。扩展实现了访问前与访问后的标题党提醒、百分比风险评分、预测解释以及文章简短摘要功能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，融合语义嵌入、显式语言学特征和领域定制评分的混合模型能够有效识别标题党，并可通过浏览器扩展将检测结果转化为可解释、可交互的用户辅助工具。摘要未说明数据集规模、类别分布、跨域泛化能力、基线对比细节及摘要质量评估，这些仍需通过完整论文进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [45] [Stochastic Sampling is Epistemically Shallow: The Dimensionality Gap Between Temperature Variation and Model Diversity in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20464)
*Izhar Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文比较单模型重复采样与多模型集成的回答波动，发现前者虽能通过多数投票提供可靠的逐题不确定性估计，却几乎不能揭示题目之间共享的知识缺口；只有多样化模型集成能稳定暴露跨题结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在重复生成时答案会变化。已有自一致性方法利用这种变化改进答案并估计单题置信度，但尚不清楚：同一模型的随机波动能否像异构模型集成一样，反映不同问题之间相关的错误模式或潜在能力维度。

Method: 作者在相同问题上比较两种设置：单一模型在温度τ=1下独立运行100次，以及由24个LLM组成的集成在温度τ=0下各运行一次。研究覆盖五类任务与MMLU、HellaSwag、GSM8K三个基准。通过Marchenko--Pastur随机矩阵检验，判断回答矩阵的特征值是否超出由有限样本噪声导致的理论边界；另构造难度匹配的伯努利零假设，并以500次蒙特卡洛模拟检验集成结果的显著性。

Result: 在每个单独模型内，各任务设置中至多只有一个特征维度的特征值超过噪声边界，表明重复采样的差异主要不含可检测的跨题相关结构。相反，多模型集成中有4个特征值超过噪声上界；在难度匹配的伯努利零假设下，500次模拟中最多仅出现1次类似结果。这说明集成成员之间的系统性差异携带多个跨题潜在维度。

Conclusion: 自一致性适合用于逐题不确定性估计和答案聚合，但不能替代多样化模型集成来识别模型知识边界的结构性模式。若目标是发现哪些问题会因共同能力缺失而同时出错，应使用来源或训练差异足够大的模型集成，而非仅增加同一模型的随机采样次数。

Abstract: When a language model gives different answers on repeated runs, does that variation reveal what it does not know? Self-consistency turns the variation into a per-question uncertainty estimate via majority voting. But does the same variation reveal cross-question structure -- related questions flipping together, the way a diverse ensemble does? We compare two regimes on the same questions: one model run $100$ times at $τ=1$ versus an ensemble of $24$ LLMs run once each at $τ=0$. A Marchenko--Pastur random-matrix test separates signal from sampling noise on both sides. Within any single model, at most one dimension rises above noise across five families and three benchmarks (MMLU, HellaSwag, GSM8K). Across the ensemble, four eigenvalues clear the noise edge, while a matched-difficulty Bernoulli null produces at most one in $500$ Monte Carlo draws. Self-consistency gives accurate per-question uncertainty but no detectable cross-question structure; only a diverse ensemble surfaces what a model does not know.

</details>


### [46] [JAXBench: Benchmarking Autonomous TPU Kernel Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20466)
*Arya Tschand,Charles Hong,Julian Walker,Nina Cai,Shangkun Wang,Suvinay Subramanian,Sundar Dev,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出JAXBench：面向Google Cloud TPU、以JAX/Pallas为核心的AI生成内核优化基准套件。实验表明，在Pallas这类文档稀缺的DSL上，提供TPU特定上下文对生成正确内核的作用大于单纯扩大模型规模；在正确性达标后，采用结构化搜索可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU内核优化已有严格且统一的KernelBench等基准推动迭代，但TPU侧缺少对应的、覆盖真实机器学习工作负载并具备优化空间的公开评测标准。作者旨在建立TPU原生基准，支持比较AI驱动的Pallas内核生成与优化方法，并降低开放研究和工程贡献的评测门槛。

Method: JAXBench包含50个JAX工作负载：其中17个来自公开MaxText库中的生产级ML算子，另外33个由KernelBench算子迁移而来，并重新设定问题规模以提高TPU v6e MXU利用率。8个生产算子配备来自Tokamax的手工优化Pallas内核，并经块大小调优，作为专家上界基线。论文使用四种反馈驱动方法生成候选Pallas内核，比较文档上下文、模型能力和搜索策略对正确率及相对XLA性能的影响；其中Autocomp采用束搜索流程。

Result: 使用Gemini 3 Flash时，加入整理后的TPU文档将单样本正确率从5.8%提高至37.3%，并在50个任务中解决48个，达到相对XLA的1.28倍几何平均加速。正确内核生成后，搜索结构带来额外收益：Autocomp在全套基准上实现相对XLA的1.36倍几何平均加速。在8个具有手工调优参考实现的任务中，Autocomp达到1.60倍加速，恢复了Tokamax专家上界2.08倍中的大部分性能；但在专用分页注意力和不规则长度注意力算子上仍落后于专家实现。

Conclusion: JAXBench为TPU上的AI内核优化提供了具有现实代表性、可复现且带专家参考实现的统一评测基础。主要发现是：对Pallas等稀疏文档DSL，目标硬件与编程模型相关的高质量上下文是正确性提升的关键；而在获得正确实现后，反馈驱动的结构化搜索是性能优化的重要来源。尽管自动方法已取得明显加速，其在高度专用算子上的表现仍显示TPU高质量内核优化具有挑战性。作者发布基准、评测框架和基线结果，以促进后续开源研究。

Abstract: Rigorous benchmarks have driven progress in autonomous GPU kernel performance optimization by establishing a shared target to hillclimb on, but no equivalent exists for TPUs. We present JAXBench, a TPU-native benchmark suite for AI-generated kernel optimization on Google Cloud TPUs. JAXBench comprises 50 JAX workloads that are both relevant and provide headroom for optimization. We extract 17 production ML operators from architectures in the public MaxText library such as Llama-3.1, DeepSeek-V3, Mixtral, Mamba-2, and AlphaFold2, and translate 33 operators from KernelBench that are validated for correctness and set with new problem sizes that achieve high TPU v6e MXU utilization. Eight of the 17 production operators ship with hand-optimized Pallas kernels from the public Tokamax library and block-size tuned to establish an expert upper-bound baseline. We evaluate four feedback-driven methods on generating candidate Pallas kernels for JAXBench. Across the full suite with Gemini 3 Flash, we find that target-specific context matters more than model scale on a sparsely-documented DSL like Pallas. Conditioning on curated TPU documentation raises per-sample correctness from 5.8% to 37.3% and solves 48 of 50 benchmarks at a 1.28x geomean speedup. Search structure yields significant gains once correctness is achieved, with Autocomp's beam-search pipeline reaching a 1.36x geomean speedup over XLA. On the 8 hand-tuned kernels, Autocomp reaches 1.60x geomean over XLA, recovering most of the 2.08x Tokamax upper bound but trailing on the specialized paged and ragged attention operators. High-quality TPU kernel optimization remains a challenging task, and we release the JAXBench benchmark, evaluation harness, and baseline results to support open source contributions.

</details>


### [47] [ExecuGraph: A Multi-Agent, Execution-Grounded Framework for Reliable Backend Code Synthesis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20499)
*Sai Deekshith Lekkala,Jothi Prabha Appadurai,Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Manpreet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ExecuGraph：一个以执行验证为核心的多智能体后端代码生成框架。其主要价值不在于所有基准上显著领先，而在于通过统一代码库、可配置工作流和消融实验，系统测量多智能体分解、执行重试等机制的边际贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次生成的代码虽看似合理，但缺乏正确性与运行可靠性保证。现有改进方法往往难以区分性能提升究竟来自执行反馈、重试机制还是多角色分工，因此需要一个能在受控条件下比较单次生成、单智能体执行重试和多智能体协作的实验框架。

Method: ExecuGraph由规划、代码生成、逻辑审查、执行评估、优化和解释六类智能体组成，并通过带类型的有向工作流协调。系统基于LangGraph实现，使用本地Ollama模型，并可选配检索模块以召回算法技巧。每次代码评估均在子进程隔离沙箱中执行，并设置墙钟超时。实验覆盖内部30题DSA集合、HumanEval的64题及APPS入门子集；比较对象包括单智能体单次生成、单智能体执行—重试基线和完整多智能体配置。论文还报告了逐智能体消融、重试预算扫描、错误类型分析与测试来源审计。

Result: 在internal-30上，三种设置的差异未达到统计显著：多智能体完整配置相对单次生成的配对Wilcoxon检验p=0.59，单智能体重试相对单次生成p=0.08；所有两两均值差的95%自助法置信区间均包含零。在HumanEval上，完整多智能体配置领先3.1个百分点。最强结果来自跨模型实验：采用DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite时，图相关类别准确率从单次生成的57.5%提高到完整多智能体的80.0%，绝对提升22.5个百分点。

Conclusion: ExecuGraph证明了执行验证与多智能体工作流可以在部分场景带来明显收益，尤其当基础模型能力较强时，多智能体分解的价值可能更高。但internal-30上的统计结果表明，该收益并不稳定或普适，不能据此断言多智能体框架必然优于简单基线。论文最可信的贡献是实验方法学：用同一实现通过配置切换构造可比基线和消融条件，从而更严格地识别不同组件的实际贡献。

Abstract: Large Language Models generate plausible backend code, but a single-pass paradigm provides no guarantee of correctness or runtime reliability. We present ExecuGraph, a multi-agent framework that places execution-based validation at the center of backend code synthesis. Six specialized agents (Planner, Code Generator, Logical Reviewer, Evaluator, Optimizer, and Explainer) are coordinated by a typed directed workflow with a bounded retry budget, implemented on LangGraph with locally hosted models (Ollama) and an optional retrieval layer for algorithmic technique recall. A subprocess-isolated sandbox with a wall-clock timeout guards every evaluation. We evaluate on a curated 30-problem DSA suite (internal-30), HumanEval (n=64), and an APPS-introductory subset, contrasting ExecuGraph against a single-agent one-shot baseline and a single-agent execution-retry baseline (a Reflexion-style ablation that isolates the contribution of multi-agent decomposition). On internal-30, the three conditions are statistically indistinguishable (n=30; paired Wilcoxon p=0.59 MF vs. SO, p=0.08 SR vs. SO); 95% bootstrap confidence intervals on all pairwise mean differences include zero. On HumanEval, multi-full edges ahead by +3.1 pp. The strongest signal is cross-model: with DeepSeekCoder V2 Lite, graph-category accuracy improves from 57.5% (oneshot) to 80.0% (multi-full), a +22.5 pp jump that supports a scaling hypothesis: the value of multi-agent decomposition grows with base-model capability. The framework's primary contribution is methodological: a single codebase that collapses by configuration into one-shot, execution-retry, and per-agent ablation conditions, enabling controlled measurement of each lever's marginal contribution. A per-agent ablation, retry-budget sweep, error-class taxonomy, and test-source audit are reported.

</details>


### [48] [Representation Robustness Under Executable Reasoning Constraints in Large Language Models for Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20520)
*Sagnik Nath,Edith Aurora Graf,Liang Zhang,Diego Zapata-Rivera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统评估大语言模型在数学问题不同等价表述下的鲁棒性，发现模型对故事题、文字方程、符号方程及同构改写高度敏感；代码执行辅助可揭示部分潜在推理能力，但无法稳定消除表征脆弱性，且会引入协议与执行层面的新失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理评测通常将数学等价的问题表述视为可互换输入，因而可能混淆模型真实的推理错误与输入接口、表达形式导致的失败。论文旨在检验模型是否真正掌握底层数学结构，以及代码化推理脚手架能否改善这种表征鲁棒性。

Method: 作者构建由数学上等价的问题组成的精选数据集，覆盖故事题、文字方程、符号方程和同构释义等形式；在直接生成答案条件下评估五个当代大语言模型，比较不同表述间的正确性变化与翻转率。随后引入代码增强条件，要求模型将推理外化为可在本地运行验证的Python代码，并分析正确性、鲁棒性、交互失败类型、延迟和成本。

Result: 直接回答时，模型在等价表述之间频繁出现正确性翻转，并且细微的同构改写也会造成系统性性能退化。代码增强能使部分原本直接提示表现较差的模型展现较强的潜在推理能力，但提升并不一致；失败会从难以解释的推理错误转移为协议违规或代码执行失败。即使代码成功执行，模型对输入表述的敏感性通常仍然存在。

Conclusion: 数学推理能力评估不应只关注单一表述上的准确率，而应将表征形式作为核心接口设计变量。代码执行等推理脚手架能够改善可验证性并揭示能力边界，却不能根治表征脆弱性；实际部署需联合权衡跨表述可靠性、正确率、交互协议、执行稳定性、时延与成本。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated on mathematical problem solving, yet prior work often treats representationally equivalent formulations as interchangeable and conflates reasoning errors with interface failures. This paper investigates representation robustness in LLM-based mathematical problem solving by systematically varying surface representations of the same underlying problems, including story problems, word-equations, symbolic equations, and isomorphic paraphrases. Using a curated dataset of mathematically equivalent problems, we evaluate five contemporary LLMs under a direct answer generation condition. We find substantial representational sensitivity: models frequently change correctness across equivalent formulations, with nontrivial flip rates across story, symbolic, and word-equation variants. We also observe systematic regressions under isomorphic reformulations, showing that even subtle paraphrase-level changes can degrade performance despite preserved mathematical structure. We then evaluate a code-augmented condition in which models externalize reasoning as executable Python code that is run locally for validation. This interface reveals strong latent reasoning capability in some models that perform poorly under direct prompting, but it does not uniformly improve robustness. Instead, failures shift across interaction layers, from opaque reasoning errors to protocol violations and execution failures. Even when executable reasoning succeeds, representation sensitivity often persists. Overall, our results show that reasoning scaffolds do not eliminate representational brittleness, but expose new tradeoffs among correctness, reliability, latency, and cost. We argue that representation should be treated as a first-class interface design variable in LLM evaluation and deployment, especially for AI-assisted problem-solving systems.

</details>


### [49] [DC-Leap: Training-Free Acceleration of dLLMs via Draft-Guided Contiguous Leaping Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20467)
*Yanhua Jiao,Tianyi Wu,Xiaoxi Sun,Yulin Li,HuiLing Zhen,Libo Qin,Baotian Hu,Zhuotao Tian,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DC-Leap是一种免训练的扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过动态连续验证与草稿引导解码，缓解并行解码中的联合概率依赖误差，在保持生成质量相近的前提下显著减少去噪迭代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型依赖并行解码提升效率，但现有方法为避免联合概率依赖误差而采用过于保守的置信度阈值，导致大量冗余去噪步骤，限制了实际推理速度。

Method: 方法包含两部分：其一，动态连续验证将严格有序的因果约束引入并行解码，逐步验证相邻及连续 token 的依赖关系，从而在中等置信度区域可靠接受更多 token；其二，草稿引导解码利用草稿结果跨多个 token 向前扩展上下文，为当前预测提供前瞻信息，并保留双向注意力的结构优势。

Result: 标准基准实验显示，DC-Leap在生成质量可比的条件下取得明显加速：在长序列 MBPP 生成任务上最高达到53.19倍加速；与 KV-Cache 结合时最高达到105.02倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过显式处理并行 token 接受中的依赖关系，而非单纯提高置信度阈值，可以有效释放扩散大语言模型并行解码的速度潜力。其训练无关特性使其具备较好的部署便利性，但摘要未说明不同模型规模、任务类型及草稿质量变化下的稳定性与额外计算开销。

Abstract: While parallel decoding is central to the efficiency of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), current strategies are often hindered by overly conservative confidence thresholds. These thresholds, necessitated by the Joint Probability Dependence Error (JPDE), result in redundant denoising iterations and suboptimal inference speeds. To overcome this, we propose DC-Leap, a training-free framework that enables reliable acceleration of dLLMs in the moderate-confidence regime. DC-Leap introduces a Dynamic Contiguous Verification strategy that integrates strictly-ordered causal constraints into the parallel decoding process. By progressively validating token dependencies, this mechanism effectively neutralizes the JPDE, enabling reliable acceleration with comparable performance. Furthermore, DC-Leap incorporates the draft-guided decoding mechanism, where the draft helps extend the context by leaping forward across multiple tokens, providing look-ahead context and retaining the structural benefits of bidirectional attention during inference. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DC-Leap achieves substantial speedups, up to 53.19x on MBPP for long-sequence generation, and up to 105.02x when combined with KV-Cache with comparable generation quality. Code is available at https://github.com/ffh-wyls/DC-Leap .

</details>


### [50] [StrideDiffusion: Accelerating Diffusion Models for Time-series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20545)
*Du Yin,Estrid He,Julián Jerónimo Bañuelos,Yang Yang,Feng Hu,Yuchen Luo,Hao Xue,Stephan Sigg,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StrideDiffusion提出一种免训练、频谱感知的时间序列扩散采样器，通过依据各频段在反向扩散中的活动程度自适应调整去噪步长，在显著减少函数评估次数的同时保持或提升生成与预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列扩散模型推理通常需要大量顺序去噪步骤，计算成本高。现有快速采样器多采用固定或通用时间步调度，未利用时间序列中不同频谱成分在反向过程里具有不同演化速率的特性，因此难以在速度与质量间实现更有效的权衡。

Method: 方法在每个去噪步骤计算频带相对能量、对数功率漂移和相位速度，据此判断高频动态是否仍然活跃，或当前轨迹是否已主要由稳定的低频结构主导。高频活跃时采用细粒度步长，频带趋于稳定后采用更大的跨步。论文还针对确定性仿射反向更新给出频带稳定性分析：非活跃频带随跳步大小仅呈线性变化，从局部理论上支持以频谱活动作为步长控制信号。

Result: 在六个无条件时间序列生成基准上，该方法将函数评估次数从500或1000步降至14至66步，最高获得18.9倍端到端加速，同时维持或改善生成质量。在条件插补和预测任务中，平均加速达到5至14倍，预测精度与基线方法相当。

Conclusion: 频谱演化状态可作为时间序列扩散快速采样的有效且具有理论依据的自适应信号。StrideDiffusion无需额外训练，能根据高低频动态自动分配计算资源，为降低时间序列扩散模型推理成本提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become competitive generators for time series, but their practical use is limited by the large number of sequential denoising steps required at inference time. Existing fast samplers typically use fixed or generic timestep schedules, overlooking a distinctive property of time-series diffusion: different spectral bands evolve at different rates during the reverse process. We introduce StrideDiffusion, a training-free spectral-aware sampler that adaptively selects the denoising stride from band-level activity. At each step, StrideDiffusion monitors relative band energy, log-power drift, and phase velocity to identify whether high- frequency dynamics remain active or whether the trajectory is dominated by stable low-frequency structure. It then takes fine steps when rapidly varying bands are active and larger jumps once only coarse components remain. A bandwise stability analysis shows that inactive frequency bands change only linearly with the jump size under deterministic affine reverse updates, providing a local justification for spectral activity as a step-size indicator. Across six unconditional time-series generation benchmarks, StrideDiffusion uses only 14-66 function evaluations instead of 500/1000 denoising steps, achieving up to 18.9x wall-clock speedup while preserving or improving generation quality. On conditional imputation and forecasting, it further delivers 5-14x average acceleration with comparable predictive accuracy. These results show that spectral evolution provides a practical and principled signal for fast time-series diffusion sampling. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/stridediff-ts.

</details>


### [51] [How Rules Represent Causal Knowledge: Causal Modeling with Probabilistic Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21208)
*Kilian Rueckschloss,Felix Weitkaemper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将Pearl的因果推断思想扩展至概率逻辑编程，提出形式化因果语义、干预定义及实现，并分析其与P-log语义的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的因果理论主要建立在贝叶斯网络和因果模型上，通常适用于无环因果关系。直接迁移至其他形式体系可能造成语义误解或不一致，因此需要为概率逻辑编程建立独立且严格的因果基础。

Method: 作者基于既有的哲学基础对概率逻辑程序进行解释，采用非时间化设定，即相关事件被视为同时发生。在此基础上，定义程序的形式化因果语义与干预操作，并给出相应实现；随后与P-log及不同类型ProbLog程序的语义进行比较。

Result: 对于分层的ProbLog程序，所提出的因果语义与P-log语义一致；但对于非分层程序以及其他概率逻辑编程形式，两类语义可能产生不同结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为概率逻辑编程中的因果推理和干预效应预测提供了系统化语义框架，拓宽了Pearl式因果方法的适用范围，同时表明递归或非分层结构下的语义差异需要被显式处理。

Abstract: Pearl famously argues that causal knowledge enables the prediction of intervention effects. By contrast, purely descriptive knowledge supports only conclusions drawn from observations. His theory of causality, however, is developed exclusively within Bayesian networks and causal models. Consequently, it is largely restricted to acyclic causal relationships, and transferring its ideas to other formalisms risks misinterpretation or inconsistency.
  This paper brings Pearl's approach to causality into probabilistic logic programming (PLP). To this end, such programs are aligned with philosophical foundations established in prior work that do not rely on temporal notions; that is, all relevant events are assumed to occur simultaneously. A formal causal semantics for these programs, together with a notion of intervention and an implementation, is proposed. It is shown that this semantics coincides with the P-log semantics for stratified ProbLog programs, while the two may differ in the non-stratified case and for other PLP formalisms.

</details>


### [52] [InferenceBench: A Benchmark for Open-Ended LLM Inference Optimization by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20468)
*Jehyeok Yeon,Ben Rank,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 InferenceBench，用于评估智能体在受限时间和单张 H100 GPU 条件下部署兼容 OpenAI 接口的推理服务并优化大模型推理性能的能力。结果显示，智能体能显著优于朴素 PyTorch 基线并常超过默认配置的 vLLM，但仍明显落后于同等时间预算下的简单超参数搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测往往具有固定工作流或狭窄动作空间，开放性任务也可能被既有方案检索和少量参数调整解决，难以区分真实优化能力与对已知配方的复用。论文旨在构建更贴近开放式 AI 工程实践的基准，检验智能体是否能系统地提出、评估并选择多样化的推理优化方案。

Method: InferenceBench 为每个智能体提供目标语言模型、单张 H100 GPU、具体优化场景和两小时墙钟时间预算，要求其部署 OpenAI 兼容推理服务器。基准包含四类场景：分别侧重预填充延迟、解码延迟、并发请求吞吐量，以及同时权衡三项指标的综合场景。论文在 15 种前沿智能体配置上进行比较，并以朴素 PyTorch、默认配置的 vLLM 和相同预算内的简单超参数搜索作为参照；同时定性分析智能体的优化轨迹。

Result: 智能体相较朴素 PyTorch 基线最高实现 8.08 倍加速，并且经常达到或超过默认 vLLM 的性能，最高达到 4.05 倍。然而，简单超参数搜索在相同两小时预算内最高可达 11.53 倍加速，超过所有智能体表现。轨迹分析表明，智能体虽能列举多种相关优化技术，但大多迅速收敛到单一推理框架，只测试少量不同配置；其余时间主要用于重复测量、修复问题或局部调参，而非探索实质不同的策略。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在开放式推理工程优化中已具备基础执行与局部调优能力，但核心短板不在于缺少推理优化知识，而在于缺乏多样化方案生成、系统化实验评估和最优解选择能力。InferenceBench 揭示了依赖记忆化方案通常只能带来有限增益，并可作为衡量智能体真实开放式工程优化能力的基准。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used to automate research and development tasks, yet existing benchmarks typically evaluate them on prescribed workflows or narrow action spaces. Even nominally open-ended tasks can often be solved by retrieving a well-known recipe and tuning a few hyperparameters, making it unclear whether strong results reflect genuine optimization or memorized solutions. We introduce InferenceBench, where an agent must deploy an OpenAI-compatible inference server and optimize the speed of LLM inference. Each agent receives a target LLM, one H100 GPU, an optimization scenario, and a wall-clock time budget of two hours. Three optimization scenarios isolate distinct bottlenecks of inference (prefill latency, decode latency, and concurrent request throughput) and a fourth balances all three at the same time. Across 15 frontier agent configurations, agents reliably improve over a naive PyTorch baseline (up to $8.08\times$) and often match or exceed serving engines with default settings ($4.05\times$ for vLLM), but still fall below a simple hyperparameter search under the same time budget (up to $11.53\times$). Qualitative analysis of agent trajectories shows that although agents enumerate many relevant optimization techniques, they overwhelmingly converge on a single inference framework. They test only a few distinct configurations and spend the remaining budget re-measuring, repairing, or optimizing hyperparameters rather than exploring substantially different strategies. This suggests the bottleneck is not domain knowledge, but the ability to propose diverse configurations, evaluate them systematically, and submit the best identified solution. Overall, InferenceBench reflects the ability of agents to operate in an open-ended AI engineering setting, where memorized solutions lead to limited improvements.

</details>


### [53] [Logic Programming Semantics for Causal Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21233)
*Felix Weitkämper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将正逻辑程序的语义与相应因果过程的最终状态联系起来：稳定模型对应从中性初始状态出发、持续不受干扰的过程终态；支撑模型对应从任意初始状态可达的过程终态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命科学中的建模问题需要能够表达因果演化与时间过程的逻辑程序语义。作者旨在澄清稳定模型和支撑模型在因果规则语言中的解释差异，并为既有的因果解释补充时间维度。

Method: 作者考察与逻辑程序兼容的因果过程，形式化比较不同初始条件及过程是否受到干扰时所达到的最终状态，并将这些终态分别与正逻辑程序的稳定模型和支撑模型建立对应关系。

Result: 对于正逻辑程序，稳定模型刻画从中性状态启动并无限期无干扰演化的最终状态；支撑模型刻画从任意起点出发可能达到的最终状态。因此，两类语义的核心区别可由初始条件的限制程度来解释。

Conclusion: 稳定模型具有更强的、基于中性起点的因果生成含义，而支撑模型提供了对任意初态下可达终态的更宽松描述。该工作支持将逻辑程序理解为因果规则语言，并以动态时间过程统一解释两种经典语义。

Abstract: Motivated by challenging modelling issues in the life sciences, we investigate the relationship between logic programming semantics and the eventual states of causal processes compatible with those logic programs. More precisely, we show that while stable models of positive logic programs correspond to the eventual states of processes commencing from a neutral state and continuing undisturbed indefinitely, supported models describe the eventual states reachable from arbitrary starting points. This also contributes to the discussion of the appropriate semantics for logic programming as a causal rule language, adding a temporal perspective to recent interpretations of the stable and supported model semantics from an explanatory viewpoint of causality.

</details>


### [54] [DecodeShare: Tracing the Shared Subspace of LLM Decode-Time Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20469)
*Zishan Shao,Lixun Zhang,Kangning Cui,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Hancheng Ye,Qinsi Wang,Zhixu Du,Yuzhe Fu,Fan Yang,Danyang Zhuo,Yiran Chen,Hai Helen Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DecodeShare，用于识别大语言模型在KV缓存解码阶段跨任务共享的低维隐藏状态子空间，并通过仅在解码时移除该子空间验证其因果作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少区分预填充与逐步解码阶段的任务通用机制。作者关注：模型在解码阶段是否依赖稳定的跨任务共享结构，以及该结构是否会影响激活引导等实际应用。

Method: DecodeShare从多个任务的解码期隐藏状态中提取一致的低维共享子空间；随后在固定干预预算下，于解码阶段投影移除该子空间，并与移除预填充阶段得到的子空间或随机子空间进行比较。论文还分析常见激活引导方向与该共享子空间的重叠，并测试在解码期评估引导向量的效果。

Result: 移除解码共享子空间造成的决策性能下降显著大于移除预填充子空间或随机子空间，表明该结构具有更强的解码期因果重要性。共享子空间会与通用激活引导方向重叠；将其投影去除可分离共享解码通道与引导成分的功能作用。相比预填充代理信号，解码期评估得到的引导信号更适合下游部署。

Conclusion: 解码阶段存在紧凑但高杠杆的跨任务共享低维通道。该发现不仅为理解KV缓存推理中的任务通用计算提供了因果证据，也提示激活引导方法应显式考虑并控制解码期共享表征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) handle many tasks with one set of parameters, but under KV-cached inference it is unclear what task-general structure, if any, is used at decode time rather than during prefill. We propose DecodeShare, a protocol that identifies a low-dimensional subspace consistently shared across tasks in decode-time hidden states, and then tests its causal role by removing that subspace only during decoding. In our experiments, disturbing the discovered shared subspace degrades decision performance far more than disturbing either a prefill-derived or random subspace under the same intervention budget. We further show this decode-shared subspace has practical consequences for activation steering: common steering directions can overlap the task-general decode channel. Projecting out this shared subspace directly separates the functional roles of the two components, while evaluating steering vectors at decode-time yields more reliable signal for downstream deployment than prefill-based proxies. Despite its compactness, the shared subspace can serve as a high-leverage causal channel at decode time. Code is available at: https://github.com/Zishan-Shao/decodeshare.git.

</details>


### [55] [PlanE: Meta Planning of Data, Tuning, and Inference for Extractive-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20470)
*Jiacheng Wang,Weiyan Zhang,Guangya Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PlanE提出一种面向抽取式任务的大语言模型构建框架，通过数据分解、指令微调和提示推理降低任务适配的数据与优化成本，并利用DTI规划器自动选择更优的基座模型及数据—微调—推理组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型获得特定任务能力通常依赖大规模指令微调数据，导致标注成本较高；同时，针对不同数据集和任务选择合适基座模型、训练数据配置与推理策略的系统化优化方法不足。

Method: 该工作构建PlanE框架，包含三个环节：将任务数据进行分解以形成适合抽取式学习的数据形式；基于分解后的数据开展指令微调；在预测阶段采用提示推理。进一步提出Data-Tuning-Inference（DTI）规划器，在给定数据集和优化目标时，从候选基座LLM及其数据、微调、推理配置组合中选择较优方案。

Result: 实验从两类设置验证PlanE有效性：固定基座LLM而改变数据集，以及固定数据集而改变基座LLM。结果表明，PlanE能够提升抽取式LLM构建效果；DTI规划器在不同优化目标下也表现出一定的可泛化性。摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标或数值增益。

Conclusion: PlanE将数据构造、模型微调和推理策略纳入统一规划流程，为低成本构建任务定制的抽取式LLM提供了方法。其核心贡献是DTI规划器，可依据任务和优化目标选择合适的模型与流程组合；但实际优势的幅度、计算开销及对更广泛任务类型的适用性仍需结合全文实验进一步判断。

Abstract: Enhancing the task-specific capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) primarily requires substantial instruction-tuning datasets. However, the sheer volume of such data imposes a considerable annotation cost, and a lack of optimization methods for tailoring LLMs to specific tasks. To address the above issues, we propose a \textbf{Plan}ning framework for constructing \textbf{E}xtractive-based LLMs called \textbf{PlanE}, which includes data decomposition, instruction tuning, and prompt inference. Additionally, we introduce a Data-Tuning-Inference (DTI) planner, aimed at selecting the optimal base-LLM and its DTI combinations for specific datasets to improve construction efficiency. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PlanE from two views: (1) across different datasets using the same base-LLM, and (2) on the same dataset using different base-LLMs. Furthermore, we validate the generalizability of the proposed DTI planner under different optimization objectives. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/gugugu-469/PlanE.

</details>


### [56] [Delivery, Not Storage: Cue-Anchored Working Memory as a Harness Property for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20972)
*Swapnanil Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向长程编程智能体的“双层记忆”框架：除由智能体主动写入和检索的文档式记忆外，还应由运行时框架自动管理并按情境触发注入“操作性记忆”。核心观点是，可靠性取决于记忆的确定性交付，而非单纯存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程智能体的记忆主要依赖指令文件、计划文档或自动生成的记忆目录，均要求智能体主动决定何时写入、何时读取。作者认为这与人类在工作中通过情境线索自动唤起局部经验的机制不符；在长会话、上下文压缩和任务切换中，主动记忆机制容易被忽略，导致关键约定、陷阱和位置性知识丢失。

Method: 作者基于记忆卸载、附带编码和事件型前瞻记忆等认知研究，提出两层设计理论，并将其映射为系统架构要求。具体实现为线索锚定记忆模型：每条记忆具有可组合的一级触发条件，包括路径、符号、语义、事件和时间；运行时框架而非智能体负责确定性评估触发条件并注入相关记忆。评估包括真实编程任务中的受控实验、会话内重复阅读分析，以及跨多次上下文压缩的记忆衰减测试。

Result: 在预置记忆存储的真实编程任务中，智能体在114轮对话内进行了0次记忆操作，表明自愿使用记忆几乎不存在。确定性注入在所有预置运行中均成功触发，且没有误报。会话内39%的重复阅读是在压缩边界前重新获取已付出上下文成本的信息。压缩衰减实验显示，仅保留在对话中的10条事实会在首次摘要后消失，并在108次压缩中的106次持续缺失；缺失记忆的智能体甚至需要检索运行时自身会话文件以重建信息。相反，由框架持有并注入的相同事实在138次压缩恢复过程中始终完整到达，即使最终摘要未保留这些事实。

Conclusion: 论文的主要结论是，长程智能体记忆的关键不是让智能体拥有更多可选存储，而是提供一个无需其主动关注、可由情境线索稳定触发的记忆交付通道。该工作强调将记忆触发和注入设计为运行时框架的强制能力，可提升编程智能体在上下文压缩与长期执行中的信息连续性和行为可靠性。

Abstract: Coding agents ship with one kind of memory: documents. Instruction files, plan artifacts, and auto-written memory directories are deliberately authored and deliberately retrieved: the agent must choose to write them and choose to read them back. Human expertise runs on a second tier that never gets written down: situationally-bound operational facts (gotchas, locations, local conventions) encoded as a side effect of the work and retrieved involuntarily when the situation cues them. We argue this second tier is the load-bearing one for long-running agents and must be a harness property, not an agent choice. We contribute: (1) a two-tier design theory grounded in the cognitive literature on memory offloading, incidental encoding, and event-based prospective memory, each mapped to an architectural requirement; (2) a cue-anchored memory model where memories carry first-class trigger conditions over a composable vocabulary (path, symbol, semantic, event, temporal), evaluated deterministically by the harness, a composition no surveyed academic or shipped system provides; (3) a controlled evaluation on a real coding task showing that voluntary memory use is near zero even with a pre-seeded store (0 memory operations in 114 turns), that deterministic injection delivered in every seeded run with zero false alarms, and that 39% of intra-session re-reads re-buy content paid for before a compaction boundary; (4) a repeated-compaction decay probe: ten facts held only in conversation vanish at the first summary and stay absent from 106 of 108 compactions, and the deprived agent greps the harness's own session files to rebuild them, while the same facts injected from a harness-owned store arrive intact through all 138 compact-resumes as the final summary carries none. Delivery, not storage, is the product: the reliable memory channel for agents is the one the agent never has to think about.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking the Personalization Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20471)
*Ashutosh Srivastava,Siddharth Yedlapati,Vinay Aggarwal,Yaman Kumar Singla,Shashwat Dixit,Jitendra Ajmera,Balaji Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SDR-Bench与SDR-Arena，用于评估大语言模型在真实双主体说服场景中的个性化能力；实验显示前沿模型存在稳定的个性化性能平台，在特定大型科技企业样本中无法显著区分成功与失败的销售触达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化基准主要衡量模型对其服务用户的适应，而非模型生成的信息是否能促使独立第三方采取预期行动。传统A/B测试和小规模人工研究又难以针对新模型重复评估，因此需要可复现、基于真实行为结果的双主体个性化评测框架。

Method: 作者将贝叶斯说服框架扩展到生成式智能体，并以销售触达为实例：模型根据推断的接收者状态生成消息，接收者是否采取目标行动作为结果。SDR-Bench包含6,279个客户成功案例，覆盖22个行业和约200家企业；评测采用时间约束模拟，以避免未来信息泄漏。论文还通过SDR-Arena及由12名专业销售代表参与的实地部署进行验证。

Result: 在多个前沿大语言模型和深度研究智能体上，作者观察到一致的个性化性能瓶颈。对于某大型科技企业样本，没有模型能够在统计意义上区分成功与失败的外联内容。实地测试中，48%的模型生成内容被销售代表评为可立即使用；资深专家评分的一致性较高，皮尔逊相关系数为0.82。

Conclusion: 该工作将生成式个性化从“是否适配当前用户”推进到“是否有效影响独立接收者行动”的可复现评估范式。结果表明，当前模型虽可生成部分具实用价值的销售内容，但在真实说服效果的个性化预测与优化上仍有明显局限；公开基准和评测平台可支持后续研究与模型比较。

Abstract: Personalization, the act of varying a message to induce action from a specific receiver while keeping sender, channel, and time fixed, has a long tradition in psychology and marketing as a two-party problem in which sender and receiver have independent objectives. Large language models remove the bounded-inventory constraint of classical retrieval-and-ranking approaches by generating a continuum of message variants conditioned on inferred receiver state, raising the question of how well current models perform personalization in the classical sense. Existing LLM personalization benchmarks measure sender-side adaptation, in which the receiver is the same user the model is serving. The two-party question, whether a generated message induces its intended action in a third party, has been investigated only through A/B tests and small-scale human studies that cannot be re-run against a new model on demand. We adapt the Bayesian Persuasion framework of Kamenica and Gentzkow (2011) to generative agents and instantiate the formulation in sales, where receiver actions are routinely logged against the outreach that induced them. We release SDR-Bench, a public corpus of 6,279 customer success stories spanning 22 industries and approximately 200 enterprises, served through a temporally constrained simulation that prevents future-data leakage. Across frontier LLMs and deep-research agents, we observe a consistent personalization plateau and on a Fortune 100 tech cohort no model statistically separates successful from unsuccessful outreach. A field deployment with 12 professional sales representatives validates the framework, with 48 percent of model-generated content rated immediately useful and senior-expert agreement at Pearson 0.82. We release SDR-Arena and SDR-Bench publicly to support reproducible study of generative personalization at scale.

</details>


### [58] [HiMe: Real-Time Self-Hosted Personal Agent Platform for Health Insights with Wearable Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21019)
*Wei Liu,Siya Qi,Linhai Zhang,Lorainne Tudor Car,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出HiMe：一个可本地部署、隐私优先、面向可穿戴设备实时健康数据的个人健康智能体平台，旨在利用大语言模型提供持续、个性化且具上下文适应性的健康分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可穿戴健康信号分析通常依赖固定规则或刚性分析框架，难以充分适应个体差异与动态生活情境。尽管大语言模型智能体为个性化健康洞察提供了机会，现有方案仍缺少同时满足本地部署、隐私保护、实时数据处理及跨设备兼容性的开源平台。

Method: HiMe围绕三项设计原则构建：第一，将数据库作为系统的一等组件，以支持健康数据的结构化管理、查询与智能体协作；第二，联合优化分析效果与计算效率，在成本和性能之间寻求帕累托最优平衡；第三，对实时到达的健康数据进行在线处理，并在长期尺度上持续建立用户模型。平台面向多类可穿戴设备的实时健康数据生态，并强调本地运行以保护个人数据隐私。

Result: 摘要宣称HiMe能够兼容广泛的可穿戴设备健康数据生态，支持实时处理与长期个体建模，并以较低成本实现有效的个性化健康监测。摘要未提供具体实验数据、对比基线、延迟指标、资源消耗或健康分析准确性结果，因此其实际性能和泛化能力仍需通过正文实验验证。

Conclusion: HiMe的核心贡献是提出一个开源、本地化、隐私优先的个人健康智能体平台框架，将数据库中心化设计、效果—效率协同优化以及实时数据处理与长期用户建模结合起来。该工作为持续个性化健康监测提供了工程化基础，但其临床有效性、模型可靠性、设备兼容范围及隐私机制的具体实现仍是评估其价值的关键。

Abstract: Traditional approaches to wearable health signal analysis, such as smartwatches, are constrained by rigid analytical frameworks and limited personalisation. The emergence of LLM agents creates a new opportunity for Personal Health Agentic Analysis, where health insights can be generated adaptively and in context. However, currently there is no open-source locally deployable platform capable of processing personal health data in real time while preserving privacy. We present HiMe, a locally deployable, privacy-first agent platform that is fully compatible with real-time health data ecosystems across a wide range of wearable devices. HiMe is guided by three design principles. The database is treated as a first-class component. Effectiveness and efficiency are jointly optimised to achieve a low-cost Pareto-optimal balance. Data are processed in real time while the user is modelled over the long term. Together, these principles make it practical for individuals to harness Personal Health Agents for continuous, personalised health monitoring for better wellbeing.

</details>


### [59] [Robust Critics: Defending LLMs Against Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20472)
*Roman Belaire,Arunesh Sinha,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: When a user asks a language model something harmful, is it a genuine attack or a misunderstood but well-meaning question? This ambiguity is one of the central challenges of LLM safety. A model that assumes the worst harms legitimate users; one that assumes the best is easily exploited. The problem is compounded in multi-turn dialogue, where an attacker's true intent may only reveal itself gradually across many exchanges, yet existing safety frameworks apply a contextual bandit treatment, ignoring the trajectory of the conversation.
  To that end, we propose Dialogue Critic Guided Sampling (DCGS), a framework that addresses this by inferring user intent at every turn of dialogue.
